Abstract: Most production ERC-4337 Paymasters rely on Process-Oriented Abstraction (POA): a centralized off-chain server signs each sponsorship request and therefore acts as a potential censorship bottleneck. We propose Asset-Oriented Abstraction (AOA), which encapsulates payment capability in a persistent, user-owned on-chain asset -- the ``Gas Card'' -- rather than in an off-chain signing process. Following Design Science Research, we implement SuperPaymaster on Optimism Mainnet. Its sponsorship validity is anchored in on-chain Soulbound Token state and deterministic policy rules, removing the off-chain signer as a hard validity gate. We evaluate gas cost on Optimism Mainnet using single-UserOp ERC-20 transfers ($n{=}50$ per system). Trace-level decomposition isolates an approximately 32k-gas delta as the execution cost of eliminating centralized signing. In pure L2 execution gas, SuperPaymaster (167,830) is lower than both vendor-as-deployed commercial samples, including a 49\% reduction against the DEX-routed ERC-20 baseline (328,937), because it replaces an on-chain liquidation path with an internal balance update. In total billed gas, the remaining gap to the cheapest baseline is explained primarily by bundler pricing rather than paymaster architecture. A failover simulation shows that non-cooperative relayers can be bypassed when an alternative relayer is available. These findings suggest that AOA can reduce the tension among usability, sponsorship decentralization, and economic efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [Stego Battlefield: Evaluating Image Steganography Attacks and Steganalysis Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05789)
*Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Leyi Sheng,Yule Liu,Yifan Liao,Ke Li,Xinhu Zheng,Jiaheng Wei,Wenyuan Yang,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SADBench是一个系统性评估框架，用于衡量图像隐写攻击与隐写分析防御的能力，包含4个核心任务，揭示了INN和自编码器架构的稳定性、领域内检测近乎完美但存在迁移不对称性，以及社交媒体上真实威胁持续存在等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像隐写技术可被 adversaries 滥用作隐蔽通道，绕过内容审查、传播有害语义、向大模型注入恶意指令，造成持续演化的安全风险，而目前缺乏统一、系统的评估框架来衡量这类威胁。

Method: 构建SADBench基准，包含4个核心任务：隐写攻击能力评估、隐写分析防御能力评估、效率评估和迁移性评估；同时评估图像载荷和文本载荷两种隐写方式，覆盖多样化的载体分布，并使用有害视觉语义和有毒指令模拟恶意攻击。

Result: (i) INN和自编码器方法在稳定性上优于其他架构；(ii) 领域内检测近乎完美且成本低于生成；(iii) 攻击可稳健泛化到新分布，而检测器无法适应，存在迁移不对称性；(iv) 社交媒体上的真实威胁持续存在，载荷要么能抵抗最小压缩，要么通过模拟训练适应强压缩。

Conclusion: SADBench建立了一个系统化、可复现、可扩展的量化风险框架，为隐写分析防御领域可度量的、安全驱动的进步铺平了道路。

Abstract: Image steganography is widely used to protect user privacy and enable covert communication. However, it can also be abused by the adversary as a covert channel to bypass content moderation, disseminate harmful semantics, and even hide malicious instructions in images to elicit dangerous outputs from large models, posing a practical security risk that continues to evolve. To address the lack of a unified and systematic evaluation framework, we propose SADBench, a systematic benchmark that assesses the adversary's ability to inject harmful secrets via steganography and the defender's ability to detect such threats through steganalysis. Crucially, SADBench comprises $4$ core tasks, namely steganography attack capability evaluation, steganalysis defense capability evaluation, efficiency evaluation, and transferability evaluation. It evaluates both image-payload and text-payload steganography across diverse cover distributions, utilizing harmful visual semantics and toxic instructions to simulate malicious attacks. Across a broad set of attacks and detectors, SADBench reveals that (i) INN and autoencoder-based methods demonstrate superior stability compared to other architectures, (ii) in-domain detection is near-perfect and cheaper than generation, (iii) a critical asymmetry exists in transferability where attacks robustly generalize to new distributions while detectors fail to adapt, and (iv) real-world threats persist on social media, where payloads either survive minimal compression or effectively adapt to aggressive compression via simulated training. Overall, SADBench establishes a systematic, reproducible, and extensible framework to quantify risks, paving the way for measurable and security-driven advancements in steganography defense.

</details>


### [216] [LCC-LLM: Leveraging Code-Centric Large Language Models for Malware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05807)
*Christopher G. Pedraza Pohlenz,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Ali Hassan,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LCC-LLM框架，一个以代码为中心的基准数据集和基于证据的恶意软件归因与多任务静态分析框架。该框架包含约34K PE样本的LCCD数据集，集成LangGraph编排的静态分析、七层RAG管道、CoVe进行IoC验证、多维质量门控，并使用课程顺序指令数据微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B模型。在43种恶意软件分析任务类型上平均语义相似度达0.634，真实案例研究中结构化分析通过率10/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件归因受限于不支持的指标和缺乏代码级依据，难以识别恶意和易受攻击的代码段，亟需更可靠的证据驱动分析方法。

Method: 构建LCCD数据集（约34K PE样本，包含反编译C代码、汇编代码、CFG/FCG等），设计LCC-LLM框架：LangGraph编排静态分析、七层RAG管道、CoVe进行IoC验证、多维质量门控，使用课程顺序指令数据通过QLoRA微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。

Result: 在43种恶意软件分析任务类型上平均语义相似度0.634，在结构化报告生成、IoC提取、漏洞评估、恶意软件配置提取和恶意软件类别检测等任务上表现最佳；真实案例研究中结构化分析通过率10/10。

Conclusion: 以代码为中心的表征、检索依据和验证引导推理能够提升LLM辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性和实际可用性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly explored for malware analysis; however, current LLM-based malware attribution remains limited by unsupported indicators and insufficient code-level grounding for identifying malicious and vulnerable code segments. To address these limitations, this research introduces LCC-LLM, a code-centric benchmark dataset and evidence-grounded framework for malware attribution and multi-task static malware analysis. The proposed LCCD dataset contains approximately 34K PE samples processed through a large-scale reverse-engineering pipeline and represented using decompiled C code, assembly code, CFG/FCG artifacts, hexadecimal data, PE metadata, suspicious API evidence, and structural features. Beyond dataset construction, LCC-LLM integrates LangGraph-orchestrated static analysis with multi-source cybersecurity knowledge to support evidence-grounded malware reasoning. The framework employs a seven-layer retrieval-augmented generation pipeline, CoVe for IoC validation, and a multi-dimensional quality gate to improve factual reliability and analyst-oriented decision support. Curriculum-ordered instruction data is used to fine-tune DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B and Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B using QLoRA. Evaluation across 43 malware-analysis task types achieves an average semantic similarity of 0.634, with the highest task-level performance in structured report generation, IoC extraction, vulnerability assessment, malware configuration extraction, and malware class detection. In a real-world case study using MalwareBazaar samples, the grounded pipeline achieves a 10/10 structured analysis pass rate, producing CFG/FCG evidence, MITRE ATT&CK mappings, detection guidance, and analyst-ready reports. These results show that code-centric representations, retrieval grounding, and verification-guided reasoning improve the reliability and operational usefulness of LLM-assisted malware attribution.

</details>


### [217] [LeakDojo: Decoding the Leakage Threats of RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05818)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeakDojo是一个用于评估检索增强生成（RAG）系统数据泄露风险的可配置框架。通过对14种LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统的基准测试，发现：查询生成与对抗性指令独立贡献泄露风险，且整体泄露可近似为二者的乘积；更强的指令遵循能力与更高的泄露风险相关；RAG忠实度的改进可能引入更高的泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在使LLM利用外部知识的同时，也暴露了宝贵的RAG数据库面临泄露攻击的风险。随着RAG系统日益复杂且LLM指令遵循能力增强，现有研究缺乏对RAG泄露风险的系统性评估。

Method: 提出了LeakDojo——一个可配置的RAG泄露受控评估框架，对六种现有攻击方法在14种LLM、4个数据集和多样化RAG系统上进行基准测试。

Result: 查询生成与对抗性指令独立贡献泄露风险，整体泄露可近似为二者的乘积；更强的指令遵循能力与更高泄露风险相关；RAG忠实度的改进可能引入更高的泄露风险。

Conclusion: 这些发现为实践中理解和缓解RAG泄露提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to leverage external knowledge, but also exposes valuable RAG databases to leakage attacks. As RAG systems grow more complex and LLMs exhibit stronger instruction-following capabilities, existing studies fall short of systematically assessing RAG leakage risks. We present LeakDojo, a configurable framework for controlled evaluation of RAG leakage. Using LeakDojo, we benchmark six existing attacks across fourteen LLMs, four datasets, and diverse RAG systems. Our study reveals that (1) query generation and adversarial instructions contribute independently to leakage, with overall leakage well approximated by their product; (2) stronger instruction-following capability correlates with higher leakage risk; and (3) improvements in RAG faithfulness can introduce increased leakage risk. These findings provide actionable insights for understanding and mitigating RAG leakage in practice. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakDojo.

</details>


### [218] [LoopTrap: Termination Poisoning Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05846)
*Huiyu Xu,Zhibo Wang,Wenhui Zhang,Ziqi Zhu,Yaopeng Wang,Kui Ren,Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统定义了LLM智能体循环执行过程中的"终止投毒"攻击，设计了10种攻击策略，并通过实证研究揭示了不同智能体的行为特征与攻击成功模式的关联。在此基础上，开发了自动化红队框架LoopTrap，通过行为画像、自适应陷阱合成和自我优化机制，实现对主流智能体平均3.57倍、最高25倍的计算步骤放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过自评循环判断任务是否完成，这种机制存在安全隐患——攻击者可注入恶意提示扭曲智能体的终止判断，导致其陷入无限计算循环。现有研究缺乏对这一威胁的系统性理解和自动化攻击方法。

Method: 首先定义并系统刻画"终止投毒"攻击，设计10种代表性攻击策略；通过8个LLM智能体和60个任务的实证研究，发现不同智能体具有独特的行为特征签名，这些特征可指导针对未知智能体的攻击。基于此提出LoopTrap框架：(1)行为画像构建——沿四个脆弱性维度对目标智能体进行轻量探测；(2)自适应陷阱合成——通过自评分机制选择最优策略和注入点；(3)技能库抽象与自反思——成功陷阱抽象为可复用技能，失败尝试通过自反思持续改进。

Result: LoopTrap在8个主流智能体上实现平均3.57倍的步骤放大，最高达到25倍；不同智能体表现出可迁移的行为特征模式，可指导针对未见过的智能体和任务的高效攻击。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的自评循环存在严重的终止投毒漏洞，LoopTrap作为自动化红队框架能有效暴露这一弱点，为大规模红队测试提供了超越手工模板的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents solve complex tasks by operating in iterative execution loops, where they repeatedly reason, act, and self-evaluate progress to determine when a task is complete. In this work, we show that while this self-directed loop facilitates autonomy, it also introduces a critical risk: by injecting malicious prompts into the agent's context, an adversary can distort the agent's termination judgment, making it believe the task remains incomplete and leading to unbounded computation.To understand this threat, we define and systematically characterize it as Termination Poisoning and design 10 representative attack strategies. Through a empirical study spanning 8 LLM agents and 60 tasks, we demonstrate that different LLM agents exhibit distinct behavioral signatures that determine which strategies succeed. These transferable patterns can serve as principled guidance for crafting effective attacks against previously unseen agents and tasks, enabling scalable red-teaming beyond manually designed templates. Building on these insights, we introduce LoopTrap, an automated red-teaming framework that synthesizes target-specific malicious prompts by exploiting agent behavioral tendencies. LoopTrap first constructs a behavioral profile of the target agent along four vulnerability dimensions via lightweight probing. It then performs adaptive trap synthesis, routing to the most effective strategy and selecting optimal injections via a self-scoring mechanism. Finally, successful traps are abstracted into a reusable skill library, while failed attempts are refined through self-reflection, ensuring continuous improvement. Extensive evaluation shows that LoopTrap achieves an average of 3.57$\times$ step amplification across 8 mainstream agents, with a peak of 25$\times$.

</details>


### [219] [SkillScope: Toward Fine-Grained Least-Privilege Enforcement for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05868)
*Jiangrong Wu,Yuhong Nan,Yixi Lin,Huaijin Wang,Yuming Xiao,Shuai Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillScope是一个基于图分析的框架，用于检测和约束LLM Agent技能中的过度权限行为，在保持任务完成度的同时减少88.56%的过度权限实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能生态系统中存在严重的最小权限违反风险——技能可能执行超出用户当前任务所需范围的高影响操作，而现有检测方法无法处理这种任务条件化的权限问题（同一操作在不同任务下可能合法或过度）。

Method: SkillScope采用基于图的分析方法：1) 将指令级过程和代码级操作建模为细粒度动作节点；2) 提取潜在的过度权限候选；3) 通过基于重放的分析在图实例化的用户任务下验证；4) 通过控制流权限约束限制验证为过度的操作。

Result: SkillScope在技能过度权限检测上达到94.53% F1分数；在真实环境中验证了7,039个存在过度权限行为的技能；在权限约束评估中减少88.56%的触发过度权限操作实例，同时保留合法任务完成能力。

Conclusion: 最小权限违反在当前的技能生态系统中普遍存在，SkillScope通过细粒度的图分析方法能有效检测并约束过度权限，为Agent技能的安全合规使用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become a practical way to extend LLM agents by packaging metadata, natural-language instructions, and executable resources into reusable capability bundles. However, this growing Skill ecosystem introduces a new compliance risk: a Skill may perform high-impact actions that exceed the minimum necessary scope of the user's current task, thereby violating least-privilege. Existing skill detection approaches are insufficient for this problem because it is inherently task-conditioned: the same action may be necessary under one user prompt but over-privileged under another. In this paper, we present SkillScope, a framework for fine-grained least-privilege enforcement in Agent Skills. SkillScope adopts a graph-based analysis approach that models instruction-level procedures and code-level operations as fine-grained action nodes. It extracts potential over-privilege candidates, validates them under graph-instantiated user tasks through replay-based analysis, and constrains validated over-privileged actions via control-flow privilege constraining. We evaluate SkillScope through effectiveness experiments and large-scale real-world measurement. SkillScope achieves 94.53% F1 for skill over-privilege detection. In the wild, SkillScope validates 7,039 Skills with over-privileged behaviors, showing that least-privilege violations are prevalent in current Skill ecosystems. In the privilege-constraining evaluation, SkillScope reduces triggered over-privileged action-in-task instances by 88.56% while preserving legitimate task completion.

</details>


### [220] [Toward Space-Based Public Key Systems: Enabling Secure Space Communications through In-Orbit Trust Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05948)
*Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将公钥基础设施（PKI）从地面迁移至太空的架构设计，包括“天-地一体化PKI”（在轨验证）和“全自主天基PKI”（在轨颁发与验证）两种方案，并分析了多运营商环境下的可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和复杂性等权衡，以及延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新太空时代独立实体运营的卫星数量激增，需要安全、近实时的星间协调，而传统依赖地面的PKI存在延迟和操作瓶颈，难以满足动态太空环境下的可扩展性和可用性需求。

Method: 提出了两种天基PKI部署方案：(1) 天-地一体化PKI，在轨部署验证机构但证书颁发仍依赖地面；(2) 全自主天基PKI，证书颁发与验证均在太空完成。并从可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本、操作复杂度等维度进行权衡分析，提供基线延迟分析。

Result: 两种方案各有优劣：天-地一体化方案在安全性和成本上更均衡，全自主方案在可用性和低延迟方面更具优势。延迟分析表明在轨信任管理可显著降低认证延迟。

Conclusion: 天基PKI能够有效减少对地面站的依赖，提升跨实体协作的互操作性和认证效率，是未来多运营商太空任务的关键使能技术。

Abstract: The New Space era has led to a rapid increase in satellites operated by independent entities in near-Earth orbit. This shift enables richer space services but also requires secure, near-real-time coordination, making efficient authentication of space assets critical for next-generation missions. Traditional ground-dependent Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) suffers from latency and operational bottlenecks that limit scalability and availability in dynamic space environments. This paper proposes architectural designs for space-based PKI that shift certificate management and validation from ground infrastructure into space, reducing reliance on ground stations while enabling interoperability and cross-entity collaboration. Two deployment schemes are introduced: a space-ground integrated PKI with in-orbit validation authorities, and a fully autonomous space-based PKI with in-space issuance and validation. We analyze deployment trade-offs in scalability, availability, security, cost, and operational complexity in multi-operator environments. A baseline latency analysis is provided to illustrate performance implications of in-orbit trust management.

</details>


### [221] [Heimdallr: Characterizing and Detecting LLM-Induced Security Risks in GitHub CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05969)
*Bonan Ruan,Yeqi Fu,Chuqi Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Jun Zeng,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了GitHub CI工作流中由LLM引入的安全风险，设计了混合分析框架Heimdallr，通过构建LLM-工作流属性图（L-WPG）并结合触发分析、LLM辅助数据流摘要和确定性传播来检测威胁向量。在300个手动标注的工作流上，F1达到0.917，已负责任披露802个脆弱工作流实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM安全和CI安全各自已被广泛研究，但两者的交叉领域——LLM集成到GitHub CI工作流中引入的新攻击面——仍未被充分探索。外部可控的工作流输入可以塑造LLM的提示和输出，进而影响安全决策、仓库状态或特权执行。

Method: 设计Heimdallr混合分析框架，将工作流归一化为LLM-工作流属性图（L-WPG），结合触发分析、LLM辅助数据流摘要和确定性传播，综合检测具体的威胁向量。

Result: 在300个手动标注的工作流上，LLM节点识别F1≈0.994，触发分类准确率99.8%，威胁向量检测微平均F1≈0.917。已负责任披露802个脆弱工作流实例，涉及759个仓库，获得71个确认。

Conclusion: LLM集成到CI工作流中带来了新的安全风险，Heimdallr能够有效检测此类威胁，该领域仍需进一步关注和防护。

Abstract: GitHub Continuous Integration (CI) workflows increasingly integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate review, triage, content generation, and repository maintenance. This creates a new attack surface: externally controllable workflow inputs can shape LLM prompts and outputs, which may in turn affect security decisions, repository state, or privileged execution. Although LLM security and CI security have each been studied extensively, their intersection remains underexplored. In this paper, we present the first study of LLM-induced security risks in GitHub CI workflows. We characterize the problem along the full execution chain and develop a taxonomy of high-level risk classes and concrete threat vectors. To detect such risks in practice, we design Heimdallr, a hybrid analysis framework that normalizes workflows into an LLM-Workflow Property Graph (L-WPG) and combines triggerability analysis, LLM-assisted dataflow summarization, and deterministic propagation to synthesize concrete threat-vector findings. Evaluated on 300 manually annotated unique workflows, Heimdallr achieves high accuracy on LLM-node identification (F1~=~0.994), triggerability classification (99.8%), and threat-vector detection (micro-average F1~=~0.917). As part of an ongoing detection and disclosure effort, we have so far responsibly disclosed 802 vulnerable workflow instances across 759 repositories and received 71 acknowledgments.

</details>


### [222] [PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974)
*Qinfeng Li,Yuntai Bao,Jianghui Hu,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Huifeng Zhu,Yier Jin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PragLocker提出了一种针对LLM智能体提示词的保护方案，通过语义锚定、代码符号混淆和目标模型反馈注入噪声，构造功能保持但仅能在目标LLM上运行的混淆提示，有效降低跨LLM的可移植性，同时保持目标任务性能并抵抗自适应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的提示词是实现特定任务能力的关键知识产权，但在不可信部署环境下，攻击者可复制并重用于其他专有LLM，导致经济损失。现有方法无法同时满足主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性四个关键要求。

Method: PragLocker首先通过语义锚定和代码符号构造功能保持的混淆提示，然后利用目标模型反馈注入噪声，生成仅能在目标LLM上正常工作的提示词。

Result: 在多个智能体系统、数据集和基础LLM上的实验表明，PragLocker显著降低了跨LLM的可移植性，保持了目标任务性能，并对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PragLocker有效解决了提示词保护中的主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性问题，为LLM智能体提示词的知识产权保护提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents rely on prompts to implement task-specific capabilities based on foundation LLMs, making agent prompts valuable intellectual property. However, in untrusted deployments, adversaries can copy and reuse these prompts with other proprietary LLMs, causing economic losses. To protect these prompts, we identify four key challenges: proactivity, runtime protection, usability, and non-portability that existing approaches fail to address. We present PragLocker, a prompt protection scheme that satisfies these requirements. PragLocker constructs function-preserving obfuscated prompts by anchoring semantics with code symbols and then using target-model feedback to inject noise, yielding prompts that only work on the target LLM. Experiments across multiple agent systems, datasets, and foundation LLMs show that PragLocker substantially reduces cross-LLM portability, maintains target performance, and remains robust against adaptive attackers.

</details>


### [223] [Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995)
*Guoxin Lu,Letian Sha,Qing Wang,Peijie Sun,Hao Zhou,Hua Dai,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型的有害微调攻击，提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR）方法，通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型，在解嵌入层构建几何瓶颈，仅需单个安全锚即可将有害分数降至10以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法在持续有害微调下可以被有效绕过，根本原因是高维参数空间的冗余性使攻击者能利用与防御约束正交的优化轨迹恢复有害能力。

Method: 提出安全瓶颈正则化（SBR），将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到作为几何瓶颈的解嵌入层，通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，保持模型在持续有害微调下的安全响应。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用单个安全锚即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时在良性下游任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SBR通过在解嵌入层构建几何瓶颈有效防御有害微调攻击，验证了将防御聚焦于低维瓶颈区域这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: The safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to Harmful Fine-tuning (HFT). While existing defenses impose constraints on parameters, gradients, or internal representations, we observe that they can be effectively circumvented under persistent HFT. Our analysis traces this failure to the inherent redundancy of the high-dimensional parameter space: attackers exploit optimization trajectories that are orthogonal to defense constraints to restore harmful capabilities while deceptively adhering to safety restrictions. To address this, we propose Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR). SBR shifts the defensive focus from the redundant parameter space to the unembedding layer, which serves as a geometric bottleneck. By anchoring the final hidden states of harmful queries to those of the safety-aligned model, SBR enables the model to maintain safe responses even under persistent HFT. Extensive experiments confirm SBR's effectiveness, demonstrating that utilizing just a single safety anchor is sufficient to reduce the Harmful Score to $<$10 while preserving competitive performance on benign downstream tasks.

</details>


### [224] [Secure Seed-Based Multi-bit Watermarking for Diffusion Models from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06153)
*Enoal Gesny,Eva Giboulot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前生成式图像水印评估过度依赖经验方法的问题，提出了一个基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的理论评估框架，并设计了SSB方法，能够在该框架的任一特征曲面上达到任意安全-鲁棒-保真度组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成图像水印技术的评估多依赖于特定模型架构的经验实验，缺乏对安全性的严格定义，导致无法对方法性能得出清晰结论。

Method: 将水印系统中与模型相关的部分与决策机制解耦，建立基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的形式化评估框架；提出SSB方法，通过特征曲面泛化已有的种子嵌入方法。

Result: 提出了一个独立于生成模型的特征曲面，能够精确比较不同水印系统在安全-鲁棒-保真度之间的权衡；SSB方法可在该曲面上达到任意所需的平衡点。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计具有理论保证的现代水印系统开辟了道路，无需昂贵的经验评估即可获得性能保障。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of generative image models has led to the development of specialized watermarking techniques, particularly in-generation methods such as seed-based embedding. However, current evaluations in this area remain largely empirical, making them heavily reliant on the specific model architectures used for generation and inversion. This prevents any clear conclusion on the performance of any method, especially regarding security, for which a rigorous definition is lacking. Against this approach, we argue that the effectiveness of a watermarking scheme should be established purely through a thorough theoretical analysis. This is enabled by decoupling the model-dependent part from the actual decision mechanism of the watermarking system. Using this decoupling, we introduce a formal evaluation framework based on security, robustness, and fidelity. This allows precise comparisons between watermarking systems through a characteristic surface representing the trade-off between these three quantities, independent of any generative model. Based on this framework, we propose SSB, a novel watermarking method that generalizes previous seed-based methods by allowing to reach any security-robustness-fidelity regime on its characteristic surface. This work opens the door to the design of modern watermarking systems with theoretical guarantees that do not necessitate any costly empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [225] [Stateful Agent Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06158)
*Zhengchunmin Dai,Jiaxiong Tang,Liantao Wu,Peng Sun,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种状态性智能体后门攻击，通过持久化组件在权限隔离下跨多会话维持攻击状态，实现单次触发后的自主增量执行。将攻击形式化为Mealy机模型，提出分解框架实现独立数据构建，在四个模型上达到80%-95%攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型智能体的后门攻击是无状态的，攻击行为局限在单次会话中执行固定行为，无法跨会话持续运作。

Method: 设计状态性智能体后门，利用持久化组件跨会话维持攻击状态；将攻击形式化为Mealy机模型；推导分解框架实现每跳转独立数据构建；实例化主攻击和两种可扩展变体。

Result: 在四个模型上主攻击成功率达80%-95%；每跳转分析验证了分解框架的有效性；不同拓扑和持久化组件的变体表现一致有效。

Conclusion: 提出的状态性后门攻击成功将攻击生命周期从单会话扩展到多会话权限隔离场景，形式化建模和分解框架为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks on Large Language Model-based agents remain stateless, executing fixed behaviors confined to a single session. We propose a stateful agent backdoor that extends the attack lifecycle across multiple sessions under permission isolation. The attack maintains state through persistent components, enabling autonomous, incremental execution across sessions following a one-time trigger injection. Formally, we model the attack as a Mealy machine and derive a decomposition framework that enables independent per-transition data construction. We instantiate this framework with a primary attack and two extensibility variants. The primary instantiation achieves an attack success rate of 80\%--95\% across four models, with per-transition analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of the decomposition. Extensibility variants with alternative topologies and persistent components demonstrate consistent effectiveness. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stateful_agent_backdoor-E89F.

</details>


### [226] [Profiling for Pennies: Unveiling the Privacy Iceberg of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06232)
*Jiahao Chen,Qi Zhang,Ruixiao Lin,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Qingming Li,Tong Zhang,Junhao Li,Yuwen Pu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM集成平台在隐私保护方面存在显著失调：平台未能从技术或政策层面解决公众隐私担忧。作者提出PrivacyIceberg三层隐私风险模型（显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合），并开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，能以极低成本（<$3）在10分钟内实现超过90%的事实准确率重建个人档案。研究还识别出六项导致隐私泄露的根因，并提出多利益相关方对策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私研究主要从训练管线视角关注记忆化导致的PII泄露，而以人为本的隐私研究尚不充分。作者旨在填补这一空白，从人类认知和LLM集成平台实践的角度，实证调查真实世界中的隐私感知问题。

Method: 通过实证调查人类隐私认知与LLM平台实践之间的失调，提出PrivacyIceberg三层分类框架（显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合），并开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，以最小PII为搜索种子重建高保真个人档案。

Result: 平台在隐私保护方面存在显著技术/政策缺失；IcebergExplorer能在10分钟内、成本低于3美元的情况下达到超过90%的事实准确率；识别出六项隐私泄露根因。

Conclusion: 需从LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者等多利益相关方角度采取联合对策，以应对LLM带来的新型隐私入侵风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized how information are collected, aggregated, and reasoned. However, this enables a novel and accessible vector of privacy intrusion: the automated and in-depth personal profiling; this engenders a chilling effect of "peepers everywhere". Existing research primarily unfolds from the training pipeline of LLM, emphasizing the exposure of Personally Identifiable Information (PII) through memorization, while privacy studies from a human-centric perspective remain underexplored. To fill this void, we empirically investigate privacy perception in the real world through the lens of human awareness and the practices of LLM-integrated platforms, revealing a significant dissonance: platforms fail to technically or policy-wise address public privacy concerns. To facilitate a systematic and quantifiable study of privacy risk, we propose the PrivacyIceberg, which categorizes real-world human privacy risks into three tiers: explicitly searched, contextually inferred, and deeply aggregated, based on the sophistication of LLM exploitation. We developed IcebergExplorer to audit privacy exposure, utilizing minimal PII as a search seed to reconstruct high-fidelity profiles, achieving over 90% factual accuracy within 10 minutes at a cost under $3, for real-world scenarios. Additionally, we identify six root causes contributing to such privacy disclosures and propose multi-stakeholder countermeasures for LLM vendors, individuals, and data publishers.

</details>


### [227] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330)
*Siraaj Akhtar,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型语言模型（SLM）在事件日志分析中替代大型语言模型（LLM）的可行性。作者首先利用高性能LLM构建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集（含修复操作），然后使用LoRA技术微调多个SLM和LLM。结果表明，微调后的SLM在问题识别和修复建议方面持续优于LLM，且计算资源需求更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在事件日志分析中虽然表现良好，但存在计算需求高、依赖云基础设施、安全风险等实际部署限制，且大多仅聚焦问题识别而不提供可操作的修复方案。SLM作为轻量级替代方案，具备本地部署和针对性微调的优势，值得探索其在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: 1) 使用高性能LLM构建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集，包含问题描述与对应的修复操作；2) 采用LoRA参数高效微调技术对多个SLM和LLM进行微调；3) 通过专家评估对比微调后模型在问题识别和修复建议方面的性能。

Result: 生成的数据集能准确反映真实场景；微调后的SLM在识别问题和提供相关修复方案方面持续优于LLM，同时所需的计算资源更少。

Conclusion: 针对特定任务微调的SLM可以作为事件日志分析的实用替代方案，不仅能在识别问题上表现更优，还能生成修复方案，且计算效率更高，适合本地部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for event log analysis, but their high computational requirements, reliance on cloud infrastructure, and security concerns limit practical deployment. In addition, most existing approaches focus only on the identification of the problem and do not provide actionable remediation. Small language models (SLMs) present a light-weight alternative that can be fine-tuned for a specific purpose and hosted locally. This paper investigates whether SLMs, when fine-tuned for a specific task, can serve as a practical alternative for event log analysis while also generating solutions. We first create a large-scale synthetic Windows event log dataset that contains remediation actions using a high-performing LLM. We then fine-tune multiple SLMs and LLMs using the LoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique and evaluate their performance by comparing with expert assessment. The results show that the dataset accurately reflects real-world scenarios and that fine-tuned SLMs consistently outperform LLMs in identifying issues and providing relevant remediation, while requiring fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [228] [Pop Quiz Attack: Black-box Membership Inference Attacks Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06423)
*Zeyuan Chen,Yihan Ma,Xinyue Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PopQuiz Attack，一种针对大语言模型的黑盒成员推断攻击方法，通过将目标数据转化为多项选择题来测试模型是否记忆了训练数据，在六个主流LLM和四个数据集上平均ROC-AUC达到0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%，并分析多种防御措施的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在展现强大性能的同时，存在记忆并可能泄露训练数据的隐私风险，现有成员推断攻击方法效果有限，亟需更有效的攻击手段来评估LLM的隐私漏洞。

Method: 将目标数据转化为多选题形式的"测验题"（quiz-style multiple-choice questions），在黑盒设定下通过模型对选择题的回答结果来推断目标数据是否属于训练集成员。

Result: 在GPT-3.5、GPT-4o、LLaMA2-7b、LLaMA2-13b、Mistral-7b和Vicuna-7b六个模型及四个数据集上，平均ROC-AUC为0.873，比现有方法高出20.6%。研究还分析了查询复杂度、数据类型、数据结构和训练设置等因素对攻击效果的影响。

Conclusion: 现代大语言模型存在持续性的隐私漏洞，基于指令、过滤和差分隐私的防御措施虽能降低攻击效果但无法完全消除风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong performance across many applications, but their ability to memorize and potentially reveal training data raises serious privacy concerns. We introduce the PopQuiz Attack, a black-box membership inference attack that tests whether a model can recall specific training examples. The core idea is to turn target data into quiz-style multiple-choice questions and infer membership from the model's answers. Across six widely used LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, and Vicuna-7b) and four datasets, our method achieves an average ROC-AUC of 0.873 and outperforms existing approaches by 20.6%. We further analyze factors affecting attack success, including query complexity, data type, data structure, and training settings. We also evaluate instruction-based, filter-based, and differential privacy-based defenses, which reduce performance but do not eliminate the risk. Our results highlight persistent privacy vulnerabilities in modern LLMs.

</details>


### [229] [Privacy by Postprocessing the Discrete Laplace Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06502)
*Quentin Hillebrand,Jacob Imola,Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了经典离散拉普拉斯机制可以通过后处理实现：(1) 对任意次指数函数f的无偏估计；(2) 输出与拉普拉斯或阶梯机制相同的分布。该机制在离散数据场景下更优，并提供了方差界、计算复杂度分析及多个应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为离散拉普拉斯机制仅适用于基本隐私保护任务，本文旨在挖掘其更深层能力，展示其作为通用机制的潜力，特别是在需要无偏估计的场景（如联邦学习）中替代连续拉普拉斯和阶梯机制。

Method: 理论证明离散拉普拉斯机制后处理可实现无偏估计（推广Calmon等人FORC '25的结果）；给出方差与MSE的比较界；分析最坏情况指数时间复杂度，但对常见结构化函数可达到线性或多项式时间；实验验证多个用例。

Result: 离散拉普拉斯机制后处理可生成无偏估计器，方差有界；可模拟拉普拉斯和阶梯机制的分布；计算复杂度在常规函数上可控；在轮廓估计、熵估计、联邦数据分析等任务中实证有效。

Conclusion: 离散拉普拉斯机制在离散数据或可离散化且能控制ℓ1-敏感度的场景下，应优先于拉普拉斯和阶梯机制使用，因其兼具无偏性、分布灵活性和实用计算效率。

Abstract: We show that an "old dog", the classical discrete Laplace (aka.~geometric) mechanism, can "perform new tricks":
  1. It can be post-processed to yield a simple, unbiased estimator of any subexponential function $f$ of the original data, giving a simple, discrete, multivariate version of the recent unbiasing result for the Laplace mechanism by Calmon et al. (FORC '25).
  2. It can be post-processed to output the same distribution as the Laplace mechanism or the Staircase mechanism with identical privacy parameters.
  Thus, the discrete Laplace mechanism is a versatile mechanism that should be preferred over the Laplace and Staircase mechanisms whenever the data is discrete (or can be made discrete while controlling $\ell_1$-sensitivity).
  We show bounds on the variance of our estimator, compared to the mean square error of the biased estimator that simply evaluates the $f$ on the output of the mechanism. Though our unbiased estimator has exponential running time for worst-case functions, we show that it can often be computed in linear or polynomial time for some common functions exhibiting structure. We showcase the properties of our methods empirically with several use cases including profile and entropy estimation, as well as distributed/federated data analysis applications in which unbiasedness is key to accuracy.

</details>


### [230] [On the Security of Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06508)
*Nanda Rani,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，发现其中41.60%存在安全风险模式，并提出了SAFE框架——一种结合代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性的自动化安全评估工具，准确率达84.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工件评估（AE）主要关注可重复性和功能性验证，忽略了工件中潜在的安全风险。由于研究工件被公开共享和复用，可能无意中引入攻击向量，因此需要系统性地评估这些安全风险。

Method: 收集509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，提出上下文感知的安全评估分类法，进行静态分析并过滤误报，识别真实安全风险。在此基础上提出SAFE框架，通过代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性分析来自动区分安全与非安全风险。

Result: 41.60%的常见发现存在实际使用中的安全风险；SAFE框架在区分安全与非安全风险上达到84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 安全性应成为工件评估（AE）的重要组成部分，以促进安全且负责任的共享研究；SAFE框架是迈向自动化安全评估的第一步。

Abstract: Research artifacts are widely shared to support reproducibility, and artifact evaluation (AE) has become common at many leading conferences. However, AE mainly checks whether artifacts work as claimed and can be reproduced. It largely overlooks potential security risks. Since these artifacts are publicly released and reused, they may unintentionally create opportunities for misuse and raise concerns about safe and responsible sharing. We study 509 research artifacts from top-tier security venues and find that many contain insecure code patterns that may introduce potential attack vectors. We propose a taxonomy for context-aware security assessment to enable structured analysis of such risks. We perform static analysis and examine the resulting findings, filtering false positives and identifying real security risks. Our analysis shows that 41.60% of the prevalent findings may pose security concerns under practical usage. To support scalable analysis, we introduce SAFE (Security-Aware Framework for Artifact Evaluation), a first step toward an autonomous framework that analyzes tool-reported findings by considering code semantics, execution context, and practical exploitability. SAFE achieves 84.80% accuracy and 84.63% F1-score in distinguishing security and non-security risks. Overall, our results show that security is also important in AE for promoting safe and responsible research sharing. The source code is available at: https://github.com/nanda-rani/SAFE

</details>


### [231] [FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06596)
*Su Zhang,Junfeng Guo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAttr，一种面向联邦学习（FL）的客户端级水印归因协议。FedAttr通过配对子集差分机制在保护安全聚合（SA）隐私的前提下，识别哪些客户端使用了水印数据训练模型。理论上证明其估计无偏且互信息泄露有界，实验达到100% TPR和0% FPR，优于基线方法至少44.4% TPR或19.1% FPR，额外开销仅6.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印放射性检测方法在中心化LLM微调中有效，但在联邦学习场景下面临挑战：安全聚合（SA）机制在保护客户端更新隐私的同时，使得服务器难以识别哪个客户端使用了水印文档进行训练，该问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: FedAttr包含三步：(i) 通过两次安全聚合查询的差分来估计每个客户端的更新；(ii) 使用差分评分方法将估计结果与水印检测器进行评分；(iii) 通过Stouffer方法跨轮次组合评分。核心是配对子集差分机制，在不破坏SA隐私保证的前提下实现客户端级归因。

Result: 理论上证明FedAttr产生无偏的客户端更新估计，每轮互信息泄露上界为O(d*/N)。实验达到100% TPR和0% FPR，在TPR或FPR上优于所有基线至少44.4%或19.1%，额外开销仅为FL训练时间的6.3%。消融实验验证其对协议参数和配置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedAttr成功将水印放射性检测方法扩展到联邦学习场景，在保护安全聚合隐私的同时实现了高精度的客户端级归因，且计算开销低，为FL中的数据所有权保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Ratio-based Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05808)
*Lena Helgerth,Andreas Christmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器学习与AI中一类特殊的损失函数——基于比率的损失函数（ratio-based loss functions），这类函数关注预测值与真实值的比率而非差值，适用于乘法误差结构的回归问题。文章分析了该类损失函数的连续性、Lipschitz连续性、凸性和可微性等通用性质，并提出了若干新的损失函数以供未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数多基于加性误差假设（如距离损失函数），但许多实际问题中误差结构本质上是乘性的，需要关注相对误差。然而，目前缺乏对基于比率的损失函数的系统性研究，这阻碍了该类损失函数在机器学习算法中的理论发展与应用。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，系统梳理了已有文献中提出的基于比率的损失函数，并提出了若干新的损失函数。研究重心不在于特定算法的一致性、学习速率或稳定性，而是聚焦于损失函数自身的数学性质分析，包括连续性、Lipschitz连续性、凸性和可微性等通用性质。

Result: 系统分类和整理了现有的基于比率的损失函数，提出了若干新的损失函数，并对其连续性、Lipschitz连续性、凸性和可微性等核心数学性质进行了全面分析。

Conclusion: 基于比率的损失函数适用于乘法误差结构的回归问题，具有重要的理论价值和应用前景。本文为其未来的算法设计、一致性分析和稳定性研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Algorithms in machine learning and AI do critically depend on at least three key components: (i) the risk function, which is the expectation of the loss function, (ii) the function space, which is often called the hypothesis space, and (iii) the set of probability measures, which are allowed for the specified algorithm. This paper gives a survey of a certain class of loss functions, which we call ratio-based. In supervised learning, margin-based loss functions for classification tasks depending on the product of the output values $y_i$ and the predictions $f(x_i)$ as well as distance-based loss functions depending on the difference of $y_i$ and $f(x_i)$ for regression are common. Distance-based loss functions are in particular useful, if an additive model assumption seems plausible, i.e. the common signal plus noise assumption. However, in the literature, several loss functions proposed for regression purposes have a multiplicative error structure in mind and pay attention to relative errors, i.e. to the ratio of $y_i$ and $f(x_i)$. In this survey article, we systematically investigate such ratio-based loss functions and propose a few new losses, which may be interesting for future research. We concentrate on investigating general properties of ratio-based loss functions like continuity, Lipschitz-continuity, convexity, and differentiability, because these properties play a central role in most machine learning algorithms. Therefore, we do not focus on some specific machine learning algorithm to derive universal consistency, learning rates, or stability results. Instead, we want to enable future research in this direction.

</details>


### [233] [CITE: Anytime-Valid Statistical Inference in LLM Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05873)
*Hirofumi Ota,Naoto Iwase,Yuki Ichihara,Junpei Komiyama,Masaaki Imaizumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出CITE算法，基于e-process的交并检验，在数据自适应停止下可控地认证大语言模型输出分布的众数，提供无分布假设的统计保证和最优停止时间理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过多次采样聚合答案可提升推理质量，但如何精确高效地控制错误水平、在答案集未知且停止规则依赖数据的情况下决定何时停止采样，仍是挑战。

Method: 提出Certification by Intersection-union Testing with E-processes (CITE)算法，利用e-process进行交并检验，实现任意数据驱动停止下的错误认证控制，无需先验知识；并扩展至置信加权投票。

Result: 证明了类别集大小无关的停止时间率，建立了主区间的匹配极小化最优下界；模拟和LLM自一致性实验验证了经验错误控制以及在扩散尾部场景下的认证性能提升。

Conclusion: CITE算法为LLM输出分布的众数认证提供了严格的统计保证，在任意数据驱动停止下控制错误率，且具有理论最优性。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by sampling multiple outputs and aggregating their final answers, but precise and efficient control of error levels remains a challenging task. In particular, deciding when to stop sampling remains difficult when the stopping rule is data-dependent and the set of possible answers is not known in advance. We study anytime-valid certification of a prespecified target answer as the unique mode of the model's response distribution, a guarantee distinct from answer correctness. We propose the Certification by Intersection-union Testing with E-processes (CITE) algorithm, which provably controls false certification at any prescribed level under arbitrary data-driven stopping, without requiring prior knowledge of the answer category set. We also prove an category-set-size-free stopping-time rate, establish matching minimax lower bounds up to constants in the main regime, and extend the construction to confidence-weighted voting. Simulations and LLM self-consistency experiments show empirical error control and improved certification in diffuse-tail settings.

</details>


### [234] [Risk-Controlled Post-Processing of Decision Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06479)
*Sunay Joshi,Tao Wang,Hamed Hassani,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究风险控制下的后处理决策策略：在保持与基线策略最大一致性的同时，满足用户指定的损失机会约束。最优策略具有阈值结构，在条件违规风险大幅降低的上下文中切换至备用策略。提出了基于校准数据选择阈值的后处理算法，在i.i.d.设置下期望超额风险为O(log n/n)，在精确安全备用策略可用时实现精确风险控制。在COVID-19放射诊断、LLM路由和合成多分类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署预测模型时，利益相关者往往不愿改变现有决策策略，除非风险约束要求干预。现有方法缺乏在保持与基线策略一致性的同时控制风险的理论框架。

Method: 提出风险控制后处理框架：给定确定性基线策略，通过选择阈值结构的新策略最大化与基线的一致性，同时满足损失的机会约束。利用算法稳定性与随机过程工具，开发基于校准数据选择阈值的后处理算法。

Result: 在i.i.d.设置下，期望超额风险为O(log n/n)；在精确安全备用策略可用时，算法在可交换性假设下实现精确期望风险控制，并提供高概率近最优性保证。实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 针对性的后处理可以在满足或接近满足风险预算的同时，比盲目随机混合方法保留更多与基线策略的一致性，为实际部署中风险控制与策略一致性权衡提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Predictive models are often deployed through existing decision policies that stakeholders are reluctant to change unless a risk constraint requires intervention. We study risk-controlled post-processing: given a deterministic baseline policy, choose a new policy that maximizes agreement with the baseline subject to a chance constraint on a user-specified loss. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy has a threshold structure: it follows the baseline except on contexts where switching to the oracle fallback policy yields a large reduction in conditional violation risk. At the finite-sample level, given a fitted fallback policy and score, we develop a post-processing algorithm that uses calibration data to select a threshold. Leveraging tools from algorithmic stability and stochastic processes, we show that under regularity conditions, in the i.i.d. setting, the expected excess risk of the post-processed policy is $O(\log n/n)$. In the special case when an exact-safe fallback policy is available, the algorithm achieves precise expected risk control under exchangeability. In this setting, we also give high-probability near-optimality guarantees on the post-processed policy. Experiments on a COVID-19 radiograph diagnosis task, an LLM routing problem, and a synthetic multiclass decision task show that targeted post-processing can meet or nearly meet risk budgets while preserving substantially more agreement with the baseline than score-blind random mixing.

</details>


### [235] [Forecasting Oncology Demand Trends with Boosting-Based Bayesian Conjugate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05270)
*Ademir Batista dos Santos Neto,Tiago Alessandro Espinola Ferreira,Paulo Renato Alves Firmino*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的肿瘤学需求趋势预测方法，通过Gamma先验和残差增强机制（Gamma-Log-Normal共轭结构）建模每周预约量，在巴西Cariri地区的真实数据上相比线性回归、ARIMA、LSTM、XGBoost等基线方法取得了显著更优的趋势方向预测精度（最高提升38.25%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗时间序列的准确趋势预测对规划与资源分配至关重要，现有方法在捕捉持续方向性模式方面存在不足，亟需一种兼具适应性和可解析性的预测框架。

Method: 将每周预约量建模为泊松过程，需求率服从Gamma先验；引入基于Gamma-Log-Normal共轭结构的残差增强机制，跟踪短期与长期趋势变化，同时保持共轭贝叶斯更新的解析可处理性。

Result: 在巴西Cariri的肿瘤学真实数据上，所提模型在趋势方向检测准确率上优于线性回归、ARIMA、朴素预测、LSTM和XGBoost，部分情况下比第二优方法提升38.25%。

Conclusion: 提出的贝叶斯增强框架在医疗时间序列趋势预测中有效提升了方向性预测的准确性，兼具计算可解析性与适应性，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Accurate trend forecasting in healthcare time series is essential for planning and resource allocation. This paper proposes a Bayesian framework for predicting oncology demand trends, modeling weekly appointments as a Poisson process with a Gamma prior to the demand rate. To enhance adaptability and capture persistent directional patterns, we incorporate a residual-based boosting mechanism grounded in a Gamma-Log-Normal conjugate structure. This boosting approach allows the model to track both short- and long-term trend shifts while maintaining the analytical tractability of conjugate Bayesian updating. The methodology was evaluated on real oncology service data from Cariri, Ceara, Brazil, and compared against established baselines, including linear regression, ARIMA, naive forecasting, LSTM neural networks, and XGBoost. Results showed that the proposed model outperforms competing methods in trend detection accuracy, with gains in terms of percentage of correct direction of 38.25% in relation to the second best approach in some cases.

</details>


### [236] [Convexity in Disguise: A Theoretical Framework for Nonconvex Low-Rank Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05446)
*Chengyu Cui,Gongjun Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸方法在低秩矩阵估计中的理论分析，提出了一种不改变原始更新规则的良性正则化框架，揭示了非凸过程中的伪装凸性，为广泛类别的低秩矩阵估计问题提供了统一的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸方法分析通常需要额外正则化且依赖特定问题的论证，难以推广到更复杂场景。本文旨在发展一个统一的理论框架，解释非凸过程在低秩估计中表现良好的根本机制。

Method: 引入良性正则化器（benign regularizer），不改变原始更新规则，但使算法等价于一个局部强凸的优化公式，从而揭示非凸过程中的伪装凸性。

Result: 建立了统一的理论框架，揭示了非凸低秩矩阵估计问题中的内在凸性结构，提供了一条获得理论保证的新路径。

Conclusion: 非凸方法在低秩矩阵估计中可通过良性正则化获得局部强凸性，无需额外正则化即可保证良好行为，该机制具有跨模型的普适性。

Abstract: Nonconvex methods have emerged as a dominant approach for low-rank matrix estimation, a problem that arises widely in machine learning and AI for learning and representing high-dimensional data. Existing analyses for these methods often require additional regularization to mitigate nonconvexity, even though such regularization is often unnecessary in practice. Moreover, most analyses rely on problem-specific arguments that are difficult to generalize to more complex settings. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for studying nonconvex procedures across a broad class of low-rank matrix estimation problems. Rather than focusing on a specific model, we reveal a fundamental mechanism that explains why nonconvex procedures can behave well in low-rank estimation. Our key device is a {\it benign regularizer} that does not alter the original update rule, but yields an equivalent locally strongly convex formulation of the algorithm. This perspective uncovers a disguised convexity inherent in the nonconvex procedure and provides a new route to theoretical guarantees for nonconvex low-rank matrix estimation.

</details>


### [237] [Relaxed Sparsest-Permutation Formulation for Causal Discovery at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05568)
*Sunmin Oh,Sang-Yun Oh,Gunwoong Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对大规模因果结构学习计算成本高的问题，本文提出基于支撑级松弛的稀疏Cholesky方法SCOPE，通过掩码零填充不完全Cholesky分解实现高效排序比较，在10k变量规模上保持MEC恢复精度的同时显著降低运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模因果结构学习计算开销巨大，现有方法难以扩展到高维场景，需要更高效的算法来打破计算瓶颈。

Method: 重新审视线性结构方程模型的最稀疏置换学习，发现精确Cholesky分解并非必要；提出支撑级松弛，在精度-支撑筛选图上搜索稀疏三角因子，利用掩码零填充不完全Cholesky分解高效评估候选排序。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，SCOPE在匹配慢得多基线方法的MEC恢复精度的同时，显著降低运行时间，可扩展到10k变量规模。

Conclusion: 所提松弛公式在总体水平上建立了在无抵消和最稀疏马尔可夫表示假设下的MEC恢复可靠性，并对排序误指定具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Despite the growing availability of large datasets, causal structure learning remains computationally prohibitive at scale. We revisit sparsest-permutation learning for linear structural equation models and show that exact Cholesky factorization is unnecessary for structure recovery. This observation motivates a support-level relaxation that searches for sparse triangular factors over a precision-support screening graph. The relaxed formulation can be efficiently evaluated via masked zero-fill incomplete Cholesky factorization, enabling scalable comparison of candidate orderings. At the population level, we establish soundness for Markov equivalence class (MEC) recovery under no-cancellation and sparsest Markov representation assumptions, as well as robustness to ordering misspecification. Motivated by these guarantees, we introduce SCOPE, a sparse-Cholesky pipeline that provides a scalable implementation of the relaxed formulation. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that SCOPE matches the MEC recovery accuracy of substantially slower baselines, while achieving significantly reduced runtime and scaling to 10k variables.

</details>


### [238] [In-Context Positive-Unlabeled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05591)
*Siyan Liu,Yi Chang,Manli Cheng,Qinglong Tian,Pengfei Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PUICL是一个预训练Transformer模型，通过上下文学习直接解决正样本-无标签（PU）分类问题，无需梯度更新或任务特定训练，在20个半合成PU基准上优于传统PU学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PU学习方法通常需要数据集特定的训练或迭代优化，限制了在需要快速解决多个任务或无需大量调参的场景下的适用性。

Method: 提出PUICL，一个基于预训练Transformer的模型，通过上下文学习解决PU分类问题。预训练阶段使用从随机结构因果模型生成的合成PU数据集，暴露模型于多样的特征-标签关系和类先验配置。推理时将标记正样本和无标记样本作为单一输入，一次前向传播返回无标记行的类别概率，无需梯度更新或逐任务拟合。

Result: 在20个源于UCI、OpenML和scikit-learn的半合成PU基准上，PUICL在平均AUC和准确率上优于四种标准PU学习基线，F1分数上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 上下文学习范式可以从全监督表格预测自然扩展到半监督PU学习场景。

Abstract: Positive-unlabeled (PU) learning addresses binary classification when only a set of labeled positives is available alongside a pool of unlabeled samples drawn from a mixture of positives and negatives. Existing PU methods typically require dataset-specific training or iterative optimization, which limits their applicability when many tasks must be solved quickly or with little tuning. We introduce PUICL, a pretrained transformer that solves PU classification entirely through in-context learning. PUICL is pretrained on synthetic PU datasets generated from randomly instantiated structural causal models, exposing it to a wide range of feature-label relationships and class-prior configurations. At inference time, PUICL receives the labeled positives and the unlabeled samples as a single input and returns class probabilities for the unlabeled rows in one forward pass, with no gradient updates or per-task fitting. On 20 semi-synthetic PU benchmarks derived from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, OpenML, and scikit-learn, PUICL outperforms four standard PU learning baselines in average AUC and accuracy, and is competitive on F1-score. These results show that the in-context learning paradigm extends naturally beyond fully supervised tabular prediction to the semi-supervised PU setting.

</details>


### [239] [Spherical Flows for Sampling Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05629)
*Jannis Chemseddine,Gregor Kornhardt,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在超球面S^{d-1}上利用von Mises-Fisher分布为离散序列学习连续嵌入空间中的生成模型，通过将连续性方程简化为标量ODE来求解条件速度，并采用后验加权的切向和进行ODE和预测-校正采样，在数独和语言建模任务上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在欧几里得空间或概率单纯形上操作，而本文旨在探索超球面空间及其上的vMF分布为离散序列生成建模带来的优势，以解决现有方法在生成质量上的不足。

Method: 1) 在超球面S^{d-1}上使用vMF分布作为噪声过程；2) 利用vMF密度的径向对称性，将S^{d-1}上的连续性方程简化为关于余弦相似度的标量ODE，其唯一有界解确定速度；3) 边际速度和边际得分均分解为后验加权的切向和，区别仅在于每令牌的标量权重；4) 仅需通过交叉熵损失训练后验分布；5) 支持ODE和预测-校正两种采样方式。

Result: 在数独和语言建模任务上，vMF路径结合PC采样相比测地线路径和欧几里得替代方案取得了显著更好的结果。

Conclusion: vMF分布结合PC采样为离散序列的连续嵌入生成提供了一种有效的新范式，在多个任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning generative models for discrete sequences in a continuous embedding space. Whereas prior approaches typically operate in Euclidean space or on the probability simplex, we instead work on the sphere $\mathbb S^{d-1}$. There the von Mises-Fisher (vMF) distribution induces a natural noise process and admits a closed-form conditional score. The conditional velocity is in general intractable. Exploiting the radial symmetry of the vMF density we reduce the continuity equation on $\mathbb S^{d-1}$ to a scalar ODE in the cosine similarity, whose unique bounded solution determines the velocity. The marginal velocity and marginal score on $(\mathbb S^{d-1})^L$ both decompose into posterior-weighted tangent sums that differ only by per-token scalar weights. This gives access to both ODE and predictor-corrector (PC) sampling. The posterior is the only learned object, trained by a cross-entropy loss. Experiments compare the vMF path against geodesic and Euclidean alternatives. The combination of vMF and PC sampling significantly improves results on Sudoku and language modeling.

</details>


### [240] [Spectral Lens: Activation and Gradient Spectra as Diagnostics of LLM Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05683)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Elliot Paquette,John Sous*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于激活协方差和梯度SVD谱的双视角经验协议，揭示了语言模型训练中损失和吞吐量无法反映的表征动力学，包括批次大小对表征几何的潜在决定作用、早期激活谱尾部对下游token效率的预测能力，以及谱头部与梯度谱对学习动态变化的表征，并通过机制模型解释了激活协方差谱与任务对齐特征学习的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练损失和吞吐量会掩盖语言模型训练中内部表征的区别，因此需要诊断工具来揭示这些隐藏的动力学机制。

Method: 使用修改版NanoGPT代码库中的受控decoder-only模型，提出基于激活协方差和每样本梯度SVD谱的经验协议，从激活谱和梯度谱双视角进行分析。

Result: 1) 批次大小是表征几何的潜在决定因素；2) 早期激活协方差谱尾部可预测下游token效率；3) 激活谱头部与梯度谱可表征学习动态变化，区分架构改进与执行优化；4) 上述现象在12/36/48层模型中一致；5) 机制模型验证了激活协方差谱与任务对齐特征学习的关系。

Conclusion: 激活协方差谱和梯度谱是语言模型训练中有效的诊断工具，可揭示损失和吞吐量无法反映的表征动力学，且谱测量与任务对齐特征学习存在机制性关联。

Abstract: Training loss and throughput can hide distinct internal representation in language-model training. To examine these hidden mechanics, we use spectral measurements as practical and operational diagnostics. Using a controlled family of decoder-only models adapted from the modded NanoGPT codebase, we introduce an empirical protocol based on activation covariance and per-sample gradient SVD spectra. This dual-view reveals three empirical findings and one mechanistic explanation. First, batch size acts as a latent determinant of representation geometry: runs that reach equal loss settle into systematically distinct activation spectra. Second, the activation covariance tail measured early in training reliably forecasts downstream token efficiency. Third, movement of the activation spectrum head (leading modes), together with gradient spectra, characterizes underlying learning-dynamics changes, separating learning-side architectural improvements from primarily execution-side gains. These predictive and diagnostic signals persist across the 12-, 36-, and 48-layer model tiers. Finally, a mechanistic model proves the main observations and explains how activation covariance spectra correlate with task-aligned feature learning.

</details>


### [241] [Fourier Feature Methods for Nonlinear Causal Discovery: FFML Scoring and FFCI Testing in Mixed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05743)
*Joseph D. Ramsey*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于随机傅里叶特征(RFF)的方法——FFML和FFCI，分别用于近似高斯过程边际似然评分和核条件独立性检验，显著降低了非线性因果发现的计算复杂度，并在混合数据上实现了高效、可扩展的因果结构学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程边际似然评分和核条件独立性检验在理论上对非线性因果发现具有吸引力，但在大规模数据上计算代价过高，无法实际应用。

Method: 提出FFML评分方法，用有限维特征表示替代n×n核Gram矩阵，复杂度降至O(nm²+m³)；通过乘积核构造支持混合(连续+离散)父集。提出FFCI检验方法，分别对连续变量(RFF/ORF)和离散变量(Cholesky分解分类特征映射)进行特征化，通过岭回归残差化构建检验统计量。

Result: 在非线性数据上，BOSS+FFML优于线性和核岭回归基线。在相同PC-Max实现下，FFCI与RCIT呈现互补的精确率-召回率特性：RCIT更精确，FFCI召回率更高且SHD更低，运行时间仅为RCIT的三分之一。

Conclusion: FFML和FFCI构成了面向基于评分、基于约束和混合因果发现的实用工具包，在保持近似精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，适用于混合数据类型的大规模因果结构学习。

Abstract: Gaussian process marginal likelihood scores and kernel conditional independence tests are theoretically appealing for nonlinear causal discovery but computationally prohibitive at scale. We present two complementary RFF-based methods forming a practical toolkit for score-based, constraint-based, and hybrid causal discovery.
  The Fourier Feature Marginal Likelihood (FFML) score approximates the exact GP marginal likelihood by replacing the n x n kernel Gram matrix with a finite-dimensional feature representation, reducing cost to O(nm^2 + m^3) while retaining the probabilistic interpretation and automatic complexity penalty of the exact score. FFML extends to mixed (continuous + discrete) parent sets via a product-kernel construction, with a Kronecker path for small discrete parent sets and a Hadamard-product path otherwise.
  The Fourier Feature Conditional Independence (FFCI) test is a fast nonparametric CI test for mixed data. Each variable is featurized individually: continuous variables via RFF or Orthogonal Random Features (ORF), discrete variables via a Cholesky-factored categorical feature map, with blocks concatenated. Conditioning uses ridge residualization in feature space; the test statistic is a Frobenius norm of the residualized cross-covariance, approximated as a weighted sum of chi-squared variables.
  Although FFML and FFCI share the same RFF/ORF machinery, they differ architecturally: FFML builds a joint kernel over a parent set for scoring, while FFCI featurizes variables individually for testing. We compare FFML to TRFF, a penalized Student-t regression alternative. Empirically, BOSS+FFML outperforms linear and kernel-ridge baselines on nonlinear data. When run through the same PC-Max implementation, FFCI and RCIT exhibit complementary precision-recall profiles: RCIT is more precise while FFCI achieves better recall and lower SHD, and runs in one third the time.

</details>


### [242] [Transformers Provably Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05755)
*Haodong Liang,Lifeng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了线性自注意力Transformer块可以显式实现半梯度SARSA和Actor-Critic等策略改进算法，并通过教师模仿训练过程首次建立了ICRL领域的收敛性保证：在合适的MDP分布条件下，梯度流局部指数收敛到最优参数流形。实验验证了模型在未见MDP上的强泛化控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer是否具备在上下文中执行强化学习算法的能力（ICRL），即模型需从轨迹数据中推断并执行学习算法而无需参数更新，并试图从理论收敛性角度提供保证。

Method: 1）通过显式参数构造证明线性自注意力Transformer块可实现半梯度SARSA和Actor-Critic；2）设计教师模仿训练过程并分析其梯度流动力学，在训练MDP分布满足丰富性条件下建立局部指数收敛性保证；3）在随机生成的表格型MDP上训练Transformer进行实验验证。

Result: 训练后的Transformer恢复了显式构造的参数结构，在未见MDP上表现出强大的上下文控制性能；首次在ICRL领域建立了收敛性理论保证。

Conclusion: 该工作从机制理解和训练动力学两个维度阐明了Transformer架构如何在上下文中内化并执行经典强化学习算法，弥合了ICRL领域的理论分析与实证表现之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of transformers to perform in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL), where a model must infer and execute learning algorithms from trajectory data without parameter updates. We show that a linear self-attention transformer block can provably implement policy-improvement methods, including semi-gradient SARSA and actor-critic, via explicit parameter constructions. Beyond existence, we design a teacher-mimicking training procedure, analyze its gradient-flow dynamics, and establish the first convergence guarantee in the ICRL literature: under suitable richness conditions on the training MDP distribution, gradient flow converges locally and exponentially to an optimal parameter manifold corresponding to the desired RL update. Empirically, training transformers on randomly generated tabular MDPs confirms these predictions: the learned models recover the parameter structure of our explicit constructions and, when deployed on unseen MDPs, deliver strong in-context control performance. Together, these results illuminate how transformer architectures internalize and execute classical reinforcement learning algorithms in context, bridging mechanistic understanding and training dynamics in ICRL.

</details>


### [243] [Tuning Derivatives for Causal Fairness in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05882)
*Filip Edström,Guilherme W. F. Barros,Tetiana Gorbach,Xavier de Luna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对连续受保护属性（如年龄、收入等）的公平性问题，提出基于结构因果模型中路径特定偏导数的统计均等（SP）和预测均等（PP）形式化框架，并设计了可实现SP与PP权衡的公平调参算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性定义多针对离散属性，且因果路径方法在处理连续受保护属性时缺乏理论支撑。本文旨在填补这一空白，为连续属性提供基于因果路径的公平性形式化与解决方案。

Method: 1) 通过路径特定偏导数（path-specific partial derivatives）形式化SP和PP；2) 建立与现有因果公平定义等价的条件；3) 提出公平调参算法，在SP（禁止路径）和PP（允许路径）之间进行优化或权衡。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据实验中，该方法在考虑PP的情况下优于已有方法，能够有效构建满足SP同时保持PP的预测器，或在无法同时满足时提供可调节的权衡方案。

Conclusion: 为连续受保护属性的因果公平性提供了理论严谨的框架和实用的算法工具，扩展了因果公平定义的可应用范围。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence systems are becoming ubiquitous in society, yet their predictions typically inherit biases with respect to protected attributes such as race, gender, or age. Classical fairness notions, most notably Statistical Parity (SP), demand that predictions be independent of the protected attributes, but are overly restrictive when these attributes influence mediating variables that are considered business necessities. Recent causal formulations relax SP by distinguishing allowed from not-allowed causal paths and by complementing SP with Predictive Parity (PP), requiring the predictor to replicate the legitimate influence of business-necessities. Existing path-based definitions are mainly practical when applied to categorical attributes. This paper introduces a new framework for fairness in structural causal models that is tailored to continuous protected attributes. We formalize SP and PP through path-specific partial derivatives, establish conditions under which these criteria coincide with prior causal definitions, and characterize when a fair predictor, one that satisfies SP along not-allowed paths while achieving PP along allowed paths, exists. Building on this theory, we propose a fair tuning algorithm that either constructs such a predictor or, when not possible, allows for a trade-off between SP and PP. We present experiments on simulated and real data to evaluate our proposal, compare it with previously proposed methods, and show that it performs better when PP is considered.

</details>


### [244] [Towards Reliable LLM Evaluation: Correcting the Winner's Curse in Adaptive Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05973)
*Yang Xu,Jiefu Zhang,Haixiang Sun,Zihan Zhou,Tianyu Cao,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SIREN协议，解决LLM评估中因自适应提示/程序搜索导致的"胜者偏差"问题，通过冻结候选集、分离选择与评估、以及Gaussian multiplier bootstrap实现过程级性能的可靠推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估中使用自适应提示和程序搜索时，若基准测试项被重复用于调优，观察到的胜者分数无法估计完整"调优-部署"流程在新数据上的真实性能，即存在选择敏感性和胜者偏差。

Method: 提出SIREN（Selection-aware Repeated-split Reporting）协议：冻结搜索后的候选集，分离逐折选择与留出评估；使用item-level Gaussian multiplier bootstrap进行不确定性量化，在固定候选集和平滑稳定选择假设下实现一阶item-level表示和有限预算网格上的有效同时推断。

Result: 在受控模拟和MMLU-Pro调优实验中，基于胜者的报告存在乐观偏差并可能改变部署结论，而SIREN始终接近有限样本报告目标。

Conclusion: SIREN为带显式调优预算的LLM评估提供了可靠的推断框架，支持过程性能曲线置信区间及等预算/跨预算比较。

Abstract: Adaptive prompt and program search makes LLM evaluation selection-sensitive. Once benchmark items are reused inside tuning, the observed winner's score need not estimate the fresh-data performance of the full tune-then-deploy procedure. We study inference for this procedure-level target under explicit tuning budgets. We propose SIREN, a selection-aware repeated-split reporting protocol that freezes the post-search shortlist, separates splitwise selection from held-out evaluation, and uses an item-level Gaussian multiplier bootstrap for uncertainty quantification. In a fixed-shortlist regime with smooth stabilized selection, the estimator admits a first-order item-level representation, and the bootstrap yields valid simultaneous inference on a finite budget grid. This supports confidence intervals for procedure-performance curves and pre-specified equal-budget and cross-budget comparisons. Controlled simulations and MMLU-Pro tuning experiments show that winner-based reporting can be optimistic and can change deployment conclusions, while SIREN remains close to the finite-sample reporting target.

</details>


### [245] [TabCF: Distributional Control Function Estimation with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05993)
*Geping Chen,Chunlin Li,Tianzhong Yang,Zhengyuan Zhu,Jing Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出TabCF方法，利用表格基础模型进行控制函数回归，实现对分布因果量的快速、准确、识别透明且调参轻量的估计（如干预均值和分位数），并在合成与真实数据上表现优于代表性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具变量和控制函数方法大多仅针对均值效应，且需要大量拟合和调参工作，缺乏对分布因果量（如分位数）的简单有效估计方法。

Method: 提出TabCF方法，利用表格基础模型进行控制函数回归，实现分布因果量的估计；对多变量结果提出基于copula的近似方法。

Result: 在多种小到中等规模的合成和真实数据场景中，TabCF优于代表性对比方法。

Conclusion: TabCF是面向实践者的高效分布因果推断工具，也可作为未来方法开发的强基线。

Abstract: Instrumental variable (IV) and control function (CF) methods are powerful tools for causal effect estimation in the presence of unmeasured confounding, yet most existing approaches target only mean effects and/or demand substantial fitting and tuning effort. In this paper, we introduce a simple method, TabCF, for control function regression using tabular foundation models, which enables accurate, fast, identification-transparent, and tuning-light causal estimation of distributional quantities, such as interventional means and quantiles; we also propose a copula-based approximation for multivariate outcomes. TabCF performs favorably against representative methods across a broad range of small- to medium-sized synthetic and real data scenarios. The central message is two-fold: for practitioners, it highlights that TabCF is an effective tool for distributional causal inference; for researchers, it suggests that the proposed approach could be considered a strong baseline for future method development. Code is available at https://github.com/GepingChen/TabCF.

</details>


### [246] [Gaussian mixture models in Hilbert spaces via kernel methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05996)
*Daniel López-Montero,Antonio Álvarez-López,Marcos Matabuena*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对希尔伯特空间中的动态函数型数据等无限维随机对象，提出了一种基于核均值嵌入的高斯混合模型框架，并开发了高效优化算法。理论证明了算法的良好定义性和在无限维空间中的稠密逼近能力，通过在L²函数数据和随机图拉普拉斯空间上的实验验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据集中时间演化的无限维随机对象（如动态函数型数据）在希尔伯特空间中建模时，概率度量的刻画（如密度函数）在技术上存在困难或定义不良，现有方法难以处理此类数据的聚类问题。

Method: 提出基于核均值嵌入的高斯混合模型框架，用于希尔伯特空间值数据的聚类，并开发了相应的优化估计算法。

Result: 理论证明了算法定义良好，模型在无限维空间中能形成稠密的近似类。实验在L²函数数据和随机图拉普拉斯空间等多样结构上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为无限维空间中的聚类问题提供了理论上严谨、实践上可行的方法，在医学等现代应用中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Modern datasets across many disciplines increasingly consist of time-evolving, potentially infinite-dimensional random objects, such as dynamic functional data, which are naturally modeled in Hilbert spaces. In these settings, characterizing probability measures, for example, through densities, can be ill-defined or technically challenging. Motivated by clustering applications, we propose a Gaussian mixture framework for Hilbert-space-valued data based on kernel mean embeddings and develop efficient optimization algorithms for estimation. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that the proposed algorithm is well defined and that the model yields a dense class of approximations in infinite-dimensional spaces. We evaluate the framework through extensive experiments on diverse structures and data geometries, including $L^2$-functional data and random graphs in Laplacian spaces arising in modern medical applications.

</details>


### [247] [Expressivity of Bi-Lipschitz Normalizing Flows: A Score-Based Diffusion Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06172)
*Meira Iske,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过得分扩散模型研究双Lipschitz标准化流的表达力，证明双Lipschitz方差保持传输映射诱导的高斯拉回在L¹范数下稠密于所有概率密度，并为基于扩散的传输建立确定性收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多标准化流架构施加了正则性约束（如双Lipschitz性），但其分布逼近性质尚未得到充分刻画。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，探究双Lipschitz标准化流在分布近似方面的表达能力。

Method: 利用方差保持扩散的概率流ODE作为桥梁，将得分的Lipschitz正则性与诱导的传输映射的双Lipschitz微分同胚性质联系起来。通过分析得分函数的正则性（针对紧支撑密度、紧支撑测度的高斯卷积和有限高斯混合等目标分布），进而研究传输映射的分布逼近能力。

Result: 得到通用分布逼近结果：双Lipschitz方差保持传输映射诱导的高斯拉回在L¹范数下稠密于所有概率密度。对于高斯卷积目标，进一步获得了无需早期停止的KL散度收敛保证。

Conclusion: 概率流ODE可以桥接得分正则性与传输映射正则性，为双Lipschitz标准化流的表达力提供了理论表征，同时为基于扩散的确定性传输建立了收敛性理论保证。

Abstract: Many normalizing flow architectures impose regularity constraints, yet their distributional approximation properties are not fully characterized. We study the expressivity of bi-Lipschitz normalizing flows through the lens of score-based diffusion models. For the probability flow ODE of a variance-preserving diffusion, Lipschitz regularity of the score induces a flow of bi-Lipschitz diffeomorphic transport maps. This ODE bridge allows us to analyze the distributional approximation power of bi-Lipschitz normalizing flows and, conversely, derive deterministic convergence guarantees for diffusion-based transport. Our key idea is to use the probability flow ODE to link regularity of the score to regularity of the induced transport maps. We verify score regularity for broad target densities, including compactly supported densities, Gaussian convolutions of compactly supported measures and finite Gaussian mixtures. We obtain a universal distributional approximation result: Gaussian pullbacks induced by bi-Lipschitz variance-preserving transport maps are $L^1$-dense among all probability densities. For Gaussian convolution targets, we further obtain convergence in Kullback-Leibler divergence without early stopping.

</details>


### [248] [When Does Trimming Help Conformal Prediction? A Retained-Law Diagnostic under Calibration Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06204)
*Congye Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了固定阈值修剪法在共形预测中处理可疑校准点时的作用机制，将其重新解释为条件作用而非纯化过程，推导出精确的有限样本恒等式，并揭示了修剪有效性的关键条件：异常评分需能在保持清洁群体评分中性的同时分离保留概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测中常通过修剪可疑校准点来应对数据污染，但修剪对清洁目标覆盖率的影响实际上由修剪后保留的分布律决定，而非仅由污染水平决定。现有研究缺乏对此机制的深入分析，需要更精确的理论理解。

Method: 将固定阈值修剪重新建模为条件作用而非纯化过程；将问题转化为一维评分CDF转移问题，推导出精确的有限样本恒等式；提出分量级转移间隙边界作为群体级诊断工具；分解出清洁侧协方差成本与保留污染成本；提供有限样本证书模板用于独立审计。

Result: 修剪的有效性取决于两个条件：异常评分能否分离保留概率，以及评分对清洁群体是否保持中性。否则，通过保留混合系数无法实质性地减少污染。给出了清洁侧协方差成本与保留污染成本的分量分解式。

Conclusion: 修剪并非总是有效；其效果受限于评分函数对清洁群体的中性保留特性。所提出的有限样本证书模板可在独立审计下提供数值保证，为实际应用提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Trimming suspicious calibration points is a common response to contamination in conformal prediction. Its effect on clean-target coverage, however, is governed by the retained law induced by trimming, not by the contamination level alone. We analyse fixed-threshold trimming as conditioning rather than purification. It replaces the contaminated calibration law with a retained law, reducing clean-target coverage to a one-dimensional score-CDF transfer problem with an exact finite-sample identity. A componentwise bound on the transfer gap gives a population-level diagnostic. This separates a clean-side covariance cost from a retained-contamination cost, governed by the dirty-to-clean retention ratio. Trimming helps when the anomaly score separates retention probabilities while remaining score-neutral on the clean population. Otherwise, it cannot substantially reduce contamination through the retained mixture coefficient. We also give finite-sample certificate templates that provide numerical guarantees under independent audit.

</details>


### [249] [Super-Level-Set Regression: Conditional Quantiles via Volume Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06210)
*Sacha Braun,Michael I. Jordan,Francis Bach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出超水平集回归（SLS）框架，通过直接参数化并优化条件水平集的几何边界，替代传统先估计完整条件密度再阈值化的两步法，解决了多变量回归中最小体积预测区域的构建难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构建最小体积预测区域的方法需要先估计完整条件密度再进行阈值化，这一两步式流程对估计误差敏感且计算代价高昂。研究者希望绕过密度估计，直接优化预测区域，但面临体积目标与模型自身估计误差的条件分位数之间隐式耦合的挑战。

Method: 提出超水平集回归（SLS）框架，通过可微的保体积边界函数直接参数化并优化目标条件水平集的几何边界，端到端地捕获复杂、多模态、不连通的条仭结构，避免完整的分布估计。

Result: SLS成功解除了体积目标与条件分位数之间的隐式耦合，能够端到端地学习复杂、多模态和不相连的条件水平集边界，实现了直接几何优化策略。

Conclusion: SLS为多变量条件分位数回归提供了全新视角，用直接几何优化替代了密度优先方法的限制性假设，在计算效率和模型灵活性上具有优势。

Abstract: Constructing minimum-volume prediction regions that satisfy conditional coverage is a fundamental challenge in multivariate regression. Standard approaches rely on explicitly estimating the full conditional density and subsequently thresholding it. This two-step plug-in process is notoriously difficult, sensitive to estimation errors, and computationally expensive. One would like to instead optimize the region directly. Formulating a direct solution is challenging, however, because it requires minimizing a volume objective that is coupled with the conditional quantiles of the model's own estimation error. In this work, we address this challenge. We introduce super-level-set regression (SLS), a novel mathematical framework that successfully resolves this implicit coupling, allowing us to directly parameterize and optimize the geometric boundaries of the target conditional level sets. By bypassing full distribution estimation and leveraging flexible volume-preserving frontier functions, our approach natively captures complex, multimodal, and disjoint conditional structures end-to-end. Ultimately, SLS offers a new perspective on multivariate conditional quantile regression, replacing the restrictive assumptions of density-first methods with a direct geometric optimization strategy.

</details>


### [250] [Multimodal Deep Generative Model for Semi-Supervised Learning under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06289)
*Heegeon Yoon,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态深度生成模型，用于解决类别不平衡下的半监督学习问题。该模型采用独立编码器处理各模态数据，通过产品-of-专家方法共享潜在变量，并使用学生t分布替代高斯分布以更好地捕捉不平衡数据的重尾潜在分布，基于γ-幂散度推导新的目标函数，在基准和真实数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别不平衡数据会导致模型偏向多数类，而在半监督学习中，基于不平衡标注数据预测的伪标签会进一步传播这种偏差。现有半监督模型虽能处理类别不平衡，但通常假设输入数据为单模态。随着多模态数据日益普及，有必要利用互补模态信息来提升模型性能。

Method: 1) 为每个模态使用独立的编码器，通过产品-of-专家方法在各模态间共享潜在变量，简化联合后验计算；2) 使用学生t分布（替代高斯分布）作为先验、编码器和解码器的分布形式，以更好地捕捉不平衡数据的重尾潜在分布；3) 基于γ-幂散度推导新的目标函数，用于在标注和未标注数据上训练模型。

Result: 在基准数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提模型在泛化性能上优于基线方法，在部分标注的多模态数据且存在类别不平衡的情况下取得了更优的分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态深度生成模型通过结合多模态信息、学生t分布以及γ-幂散度目标函数，有效解决了类别不平衡下的半监督学习问题，为多模态数据在不平衡场景下的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: When modeling class-imbalanced data, it is crucial to address the imbalance, as models trained on such data tend to be biased towards the majority classes. This problem is amplified under partial supervision, where pseudo-labels for unlabeled data are predicted based on imbalanced labeled data, propagating the bias. While recent semi-supervised models address class imbalance, they typically assume single-modal input data. However, with the growing availability of multimodal data, it is essential to leverage complementary modalities. In this article, we propose a multimodal deep generative model for semi-supervised learning under class imbalance. Our approach uses separate encoders for each modality, sharing latent variables across modalities, and simplifies joint posterior computation with a product-of-experts method. To further address class imbalance, we replace typical Gaussian distributions with Student's t-distributions for the prior, encoder, and decoder, better capturing the heavy-tailed latent distributions in imbalanced data. We derive a new objective function for training the proposed model on both labeled and unlabeled data using $γ$-power divergence. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms baseline methods in generalization, achieving superior classification performance for partially labeled multimodal data with imbalanced class distributions.

</details>


### [251] [End-to-End Identifiable and Consistent Recurrent Switching Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06315)
*Carles Balsells-Rodas,Zhengrui Xiang,Xavier Sumba,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对深度生成模型中可识别表示学习的挑战，特别是具有状态切换动力学的序列数据。现有方法存在严格假设限制（如平稳性）和VAE估计器的近似间隙问题。作者在理论上扩展了可识别性条件，并提出基于流的估计器ΩSDS，通过期望最大化实现精确似然优化。实验表明ΩSDS在解缠和质量预测上优于VAE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型中对序列数据中具有状态切换动力学的潜在表示进行可识别学习仍存在根本性挑战，现有方法受限于严格的假设（如平稳性或有限发射模型）以及VAE估计器带来的近似间隙，限制了潜在结构的恢复。

Method: 1) 理论上建立了一类广泛的递归非线性切换动态系统在灵活假设下的可识别性；2) 提出了ΩSDS，一种基于流的估计器，利用期望最大化算法实现精确似然优化。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，ΩSDS相比基于VAE的估计器实现了更好的解缠效果和更准确的基础动力学预测。

Conclusion: 本文同时解决了理论（可识别性）和实践（估计器近似间隙）两方面的局限性，为非线性切换动态系统的可识别表示学习提供了更一般的理论保证和更有效的估计方法。

Abstract: Learning identifiable representations in deep generative models remains a fundamental challenge, particularly for sequential data with regime-switching dynamics. Existing approaches establish identifiability under restrictive assumptions, such as stationarity or limited emission models, and typically rely on variational autoencoder (VAE) estimators, which introduce approximation gaps that limit the recovery of the latent structure. In this work, we address both the theoretical and practical limitations of this setting. First, we establish identifiability of a broad class of recurrent nonlinear switching dynamical systems under flexible assumptions, significantly extending prior results. Second, we introduce $Ω$SDS, a flow-based estimator that enables exact likelihood optimization using expectation-maximisation. Through empirical validation on both synthetic and real-world data, our results demonstrate that $Ω$SDS achieves improved disentanglement compared to VAE-based estimators and more accurate forecasting of underlying dynamics.

</details>


### [252] [The Interplay of Data Structure and Imbalance in the Learning Dynamics of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06367)
*Flavio Nicoletti,Chenxiao Ma,Enrico Ventura,Luca Saglietti,Stefano Sarao Mannelli*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建高维分析框架，研究基于得分的扩散模型在异质数据（类别不平衡、结构差异）下的训练动力学。通过分析随机特征模型在高斯混合数据上的行为，揭示了类别方差主导学习顺序、质心几何次之的层级结构，并发现采样不平衡可逆转该顺序，导致少数类别在反向扩散中出现延迟的“分化时间”。理论预测在Fashion MNIST上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集本质上是异质的，但不同类别间的结构差异和采样不平衡如何影响扩散模型的训练动态，以及是否可能加剧模型表现差异，目前尚不清楚。现有理论假设数据同质，无法解释类别不平衡和异质性如何重塑这些动态。

Method: 开发高维分析框架，研究基于得分的扩散模型中类别依赖的学习行为。分析在高斯混合数据上训练的随机特征模型，推导特征-协方差谱以刻画各类别的泛化和记忆时间。

Result: 揭示了控制学习动态的显式层级结构：类别方差是学习顺序的主要决定因素（高方差类别优先学习），质心几何起次要作用；采样不平衡作为调制器可逆转此顺序，在强不平衡下迫使少数类别在反向扩散中获得延迟的、独特的分化时间。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可能在部分类别上发生过拟合记忆，而其他类别尚未充分学习。理论预测在Fashion MNIST数据集上使用U-Net模型得到实证验证。

Abstract: Real-world datasets are inherently heterogeneous, yet how per-class structural differences and sampling imbalance shape the training dynamics of diffusion models-and potentially exacerbate disparities-remains poorly understood. While models typically transition from an initial phase of generalization to memorizing the training set, existing theory assumes homogeneous data, leaving open how class imbalance and heterogeneity reshape these dynamics.
