In this work, we develop a high-dimensional analytical framework to study class-dependent learning in score-based diffusion models. Analyzing a random-features model trained on Gaussian mixtures, we derive the feature-covariance spectrum to characterize per-class generalization and memorization times. We reveal the explicit hierarchy governing these dynamics: class variance is the primary determinant of learning order-consistently favoring higher-variance classes-while centroid geometry plays a secondary role. Sampling imbalance acts as a modulator that can reverse this ordering and, under strong imbalance, forces minority classes to acquire distinct, delayed speciation times during backward diffusion. Together, these results suggest that diffusion models can memorize some classes while others remain insufficiently learned. We validate our theoretical predictions empirically using U-Net models trained on Fashion MNIST.

</details>


### [253] [DARTS: Targeting Prognostic Covariates in Budget-Constrained Sequential Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06608)
*Kateryna Husar,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DARTS通过Thompson采样的序贯优化方法，在预算约束下自适应选择高预测性协变量进行重新随机化和回归调整，在保证批次级随机化有效性的同时显著提升因果推断效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机对照试验假设预后协变量已知且获取无成本，但实际中高维预处理数据获取成本高昂，需在协变量自适应精度与测量预算之间进行权衡。

Method: 将协变量获取视为嵌入在设计因果推断任务中的序贯优化问题，利用预算约束的组合Thompson采样器跨连续批次学习最具预测性的协变量，基于所选协变量执行重新随机化和回归调整以降低批次级平均处理效应方差。

Result: 推导了自适应协变量选择的解耦结果（保证批次级随机化有效性）和累积逆方差加权估计量的渐近覆盖性质，推导了匹配极小化最优下界（对数因子内）的贝叶斯风险界。实验表明DARTS系统性地将预算集中在信息丰富的特征上，显著缩小与Oracle设计的效率差距。

Conclusion: DARTS在严格保持推断有效性的前提下，通过预算驱动的自适应协变量选择显著提升了因果推断效率。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials typically assume that prognostic covariates are known and available at no cost. In practice, obtaining high-dimensional pretreatment data is costly, forcing a trade-off between covariate-adaptive precision and a measurement budget. We introduce Dynamic Adaptive Rerandomization via Thompson Sampling (DARTS), which treats covariate acquisition as a sequential optimization problem embedded within a design-based causal inference task. A budgeted combinatorial Thompson sampler learns which covariates are most prognostic across successive batches; selected covariates then drive rerandomization and regression adjustment to reduce batch-level average treatment effect variance. Our primary theoretical contribution is a decoupling result: adaptive covariate selection based on past batches preserves batch-level randomization validity, and the cumulative inverse-variance weighted estimator achieves at least nominal asymptotic coverage. We further derive a Bayes risk bound for the acquisition layer that matches the minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, DARTS systematically concentrates the budget on informative features, significantly closing the efficiency gap to oracle designs while maintaining strict inferential validity.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Optimal Confidence Band for Kernel Gradient Flow Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05768)
*Yuqian Cheng,Zhuo Chen,Qian Lin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了核梯度流在最大范数下的泛化误差和均匀推断，在容量-源条件框架下建立了连续和离散核梯度流的收敛速度，证明其达到极小极大最优速率，并构建了最优宽度的同时置信带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核回归方法中，对于核梯度流这类特定方法的最大范数泛化误差和均匀推断（如同时置信带）的理论性质尚不明确，需要建立相应的理论保证。

Method: 在容量-源条件框架下，利用核函数的嵌入指数α₀，分析连续和离散核梯度流在源条件s>α₀时的最大范数泛化误差收敛速度，并基于此构造同时置信带。

Result: 建立了连续和离散核梯度流在最大范数下的收敛速度，证明其与极小极大最优速率匹配；构造的同时置信带宽度缩减速率大于且可任意接近极小极大最优速率。

Conclusion: 核梯度流在最大范数泛化误差和同时置信带两方面均达到或逼近最优理论界，为核回归方法的统计推断提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the supremum-norm generalization error and the uniform inference for a specific class of kernel regression methods, namely the kernel gradient flows. Under the widely adopted capacity-source condition framework in the kernel regression literature, we first establish convergence rates for the supremum norm generalization error of both continuous and discrete kernel gradient flows under the source condition $s>α_0$, where $α_0\in(0,1)$ denotes the embedding index of the kernel function. Moreover, we show that these rates match the minimax optimal rates. Building on this result, we then construct simultaneous confidence bands for both continuous and discrete kernel gradient flows. Notably, the widths of the proposed confidence bands are also optimal, in the sense that their shrinkage rates are greater than, while can be arbitrarily close to, the minimax optimal rates.

</details>


### [255] [Time-Inhomogeneous Preconditioned Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06091)
*Alexander Falk,Laurenz Nagler,Andreas Habring,Thomas Pock*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出TIPreL方法，通过引入时间和位置依赖的预条件子，同时解决Langevin采样中的全局模式覆盖和局部模式探索两大挑战，在理论和实验上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langevin采样面临两个主要挑战：(1) 多峰分布中不同模态之间的全局模式覆盖；(2) 势函数Ψ具有多样且病态的局部模态几何结构时，局部模式探索困难。现有预条件方法在全局模式覆盖和局部模式探索之间存在内在权衡，无法同时解决两者。

Method: 提出TIPreL方法，引入时间和位置依赖的预条件子，将全局和局部信息统一到单一框架中。在连续时间和驯化Euler离散化下证明了Wasserstein-2距离下的收敛性，分析扩展到了时间和空间依赖的扩散系数以及仅局部Lipschitz的漂移项。

Result: 在二维严重病态示例和更高维的贝叶斯逻辑回归任务上，TIPreL在实验上优于其他竞争性预条件方案，验证了方法的效率。

Conclusion: TIPreL通过时间和位置依赖的预条件子设计，有效解决了Langevin采样中全局模式覆盖与局部模式探索之间的权衡问题，并在理论和实验上均取得了优于现有方法的结果。

Abstract: Langevin sampling from distributions of the form $p(x) \propto \exp(-Ψ(x))$ faces two major challenges: (global) mode coverage and (local) mode exploration. The first challenge is particularly relevant for multi-modal distributions with disjoint modes, whereas the second arises when the potential $Ψ$ exhibits diverse and ill-conditioned local mode geometry. To address these challenges, a common approach is to precondition Langevin dynamics with problem-specific information, such as the sample covariance or the local curvature of $Ψ$. However, existing preconditioner choices inherently involve a trade-off between global mode coverage and local mode exploration, and no prior method resolves both simultaneously. To overcome this limitation, we propose the TIPreL, which introduces a time- and position-dependent preconditioner. This design effectively addresses both challenges mentioned above within a single framework. We establish convergence of the resulting dynamics in the Wasserstein-2 distance both in continuous time and for a tamed Euler discretization. In particular, our analysis extends the existing state of the art by proving convergence under time- and space-dependent diffusion coefficients, and only locally Lipschitz drifts, which has not been covered by prior work. Finally, we experimentally compare TIPreL with competing preconditioning schemes on a two-dimensional, severely ill-posed example and on a Bayesian logistic regression task in higher dimensions, confirming the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [256] [Minimax estimation of Functional Principal Components from noisy discretized functional data: the case of smooth processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06417)
*Nassim Bourarach,Franck Picard,Vincent Rivoirard,Angelina Roche*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数主成分分析中协方差特征函数和特征值的极小极大估计。在任意Hölder光滑度且无参数化特征值衰减假设下，揭示了核光滑度与局部谱分离的不同作用，推导了非渐近极小极大下界，并构建了基于Coiflet小波的投影估计器，在样本量和网格分辨率上达到最优依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协方差特征估计的理论缺乏对核光滑度、离散化和谱结构三者联合作用的系统理解，特别是核正则性本身不足以保证特征函数估计的一致性，需要刻画光滑性与谱分离之间的交互作用。

Method: 引入联合控制Hölder光滑度和局部相对逆特征间隙的过程类，推导非渐近极小极大下界；构造基于Coiflet尺度函数的小波投影估计器及适用于任意Hölder光滑度的求积方案。

Result: 特征函数估计的极小极大速率为δ_ℓ n^{-1}+p^{-2α}，其中δ_ℓ量化谱难度；所提估计器在样本量和网格分辨率上匹配极小极大依赖；通过Karhunen–Loève准则联系谱衰减、特征函数正则性和协方差核光滑度。

Conclusion: 核光滑度过低类别不能保证极小极大一致估计；所提框架涵盖经典高斯过程和Karhunen–Loève构造，模拟验证了相变和离散化效应。

Abstract: We study the minimax estimation of covariance eigenfunctions and eigenvalues in functional principal component analysis when $n$ trajectories are observed at $p$ common grid points with additive noise. We consider covariance kernels with arbitrary Hölder smoothness and no prescribed parametric decay of the eigenvalues. In this setting, kernel smoothness and local spectral separation play distinct roles: a minimax inconsistency result over the smoothness-only class shows that kernel regularity alone is not sufficient for minimax-consistent eigenfunction estimation. To capture this interplay, we introduce a class of processes that jointly controls the Hölder smoothness of the covariance kernel and a local relative inverse eigengap quantity at the target index $\ell$. Over this class, we derive non-asymptotic minimax lower bounds for eigenfunction estimation that disentangle sampling variability, discretization and spectral effects, revealing rates of order $δ_\ell n^{-1}+p^{-2α}$, where $δ_\ell$ quantifies the spectral difficulty. We also obtain non-asymptotic lower bounds for eigenvalue estimation under a relative squared-error loss. We then construct a computable wavelet projection estimator based on Coiflet scaling functions and a quadrature scheme designed to accommodate arbitrary Hölder smoothness. For eigenfunction estimation, this estimator matches the minimax dependence on the sample size and grid resolution, up to the natural spectral factor, for any Hölder index $α>0$. Finally, we show that the proposed framework covers several classical Gaussian processes and Karhunen--Loève constructions. In particular, a Karhunen--Loève based criterion links spectral decay, eigenfunction regularity and covariance-kernel smoothness, and yields controlled simulation settings illustrating the predicted phase transitions and least-favourable discretization effects.

</details>


### [257] [Time-sensitive anytime-valid testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06521)
*Eugenio Clerico,Tobias Wegel,Iskander Azangulov,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“时间敏感测试-赌博”框架，通过奖励早期拒绝并最大化其期望值，在控制I类错误的同时优先快速决策，并给出了最优控制问题的贝尔曼表示，推导了硬截止和指数衰减奖励下的最优e过程，提出EDO准则作为GRO准则的有限时间尺度对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用中，只有在足够早地拒绝原假设时，拒绝才是有意义的。现有任意有效检验方法允许在数据收集过程中持续检验，但缺乏对拒绝时间的奖励机制，因此需要引入时间敏感性。

Method: 采用测试-赌博框架，将早期拒绝奖励纳入期望值最大化问题，转化为最优控制问题，利用贝尔曼方程简化表示；针对硬截止期限简化为有限时域Neyman-Pearson问题；对指数衰减奖励给出平稳近似，导出EDO准则。

Result: 证明了最优控制问题可用时间和证据（而非完整历史）的贝尔曼表示；硬截止下简单vs简单假设归约为有限时域Neyman-Pearson问题并得到最优e过程；指数衰减奖励下得到EDO准则，并在大时间尺度极限下恢复GRO准则。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一的时间敏感任意有效检验框架，将早期拒绝偏好纳入最优设计，EDO准则为GRO准则提供了有限时间尺度的自然对应，丰富了任意有效统计的理论工具。

Abstract: Anytime-valid tests allow evidence to be checked during data collection: one can either continue testing or stop and reject the null while still controlling type-I error. Yet, in many applications rejection is useful only if it comes soon enough. We introduce a time-sensitive testing-by-betting framework that favours early rejection by assigning rewards to rejection times and maximising their expected value under a given alternative. This encompasses hard deadlines and softer time preferences. The resulting optimal control problem admits a Bellman representation in terms only of time and evidence against the null, rather than the full history. For hard deadlines, the simple-vs-simple case reduces to a finite-horizon Neyman--Pearson problem and identify the corresponding optimal e-process. Furthermore, we show that exponentially decaying rewards admit a stationary approximation, yielding the exponential-decay-optimal (EDO) criterion: a finite-time-scale counterpart to the classical growth-rate-optimal (GRO) viewpoint in anytime-valid statistics, with the GRO criterion recovered in the large-time-scale limit.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半空间（halfspaces）在细粒度均匀收敛行为上的表现，超越了最坏情况的VC界。对于高维（d≥2）非齐次半空间，标准一阶VC界本质上是紧的；而对于二维齐次半空间，则展现出显著不同的行为，可实现情况下误差为O(1/n)，不可知情况下通过带状分析得到仅含ln ln n开销的偏差界，且该开销是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VC维理论给出的均匀收敛界往往是宽松的最坏情况估计，无法捕捉不同结构和维度下半空间学习问题的真实复杂度。本文旨在揭示半空间在细粒度层面的收敛行为，刻画维度与结构（齐次/非齐次）对收敛速率的关键影响。

Method: 对于高维非齐次半空间，采用标准一阶VC分析方法证明其紧性；对于二维齐次半空间，在可实现情形下直接分析一致性假设的误差界，在不可知情形下提出了一种关键楔形局部化（critical-wedge localization）论证方法，在每一戴adic风险带上建立无对数项的偏差界，再通过对数对数重叠（ln ln n）的并集界整合所有带。

Result: 高维非齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为Θ(d ln(n/d)/n)，不可知情形下偏差随真实误差τ以√(τ ln(1/τ))尺度变化。二维齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为O(1/n)；不可知情形下得到带状无对数偏差界，整体并集开销仅为ln ln n，且匹配下界证明该开销不可避免。

Conclusion: 本文为半空间的均匀收敛提供了细粒度且近乎完整的刻画，揭示了维度（二维与高维）和结构（齐次与非齐次）之间清晰的阈值效应，说明超越最坏情况VC界的研究对于深入理解学习问题的本质复杂度至关重要。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [259] [A Unified Pair-GRPO Family: From Implicit to Explicit Preference Constraints for Stable and General RL Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06375)
*Hao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pair-GRPO系列方法（包括Soft-Pair-GRPO和Hard-Pair-GRPO），从理论上统一了基于偏好的RL优化框架，解决了RLHF中策略更新不稳定、梯度方向模糊、可解释性差和高梯度方差等问题。Soft-Pair-GRPO将GRPO的组归一化标量奖励替换为二元成对偏好奖励，并证明了其梯度等价定理；Hard-Pair-GRPO引入显式局部概率约束和约束KL拟合优化。理论和实验均证明了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过RLHF进行对齐时，主流成对偏好学习范式存在策略更新不稳定、梯度方向模糊、可解释性差和梯度方差高等系统性问题，亟需一个统一的理论框架来系统性解决这些局限。

Method: 提出Pair-GRPO系列：Soft-Pair-GRPO将GRPO的组归一化标量奖励替换为二元成对偏好奖励，并保留裁剪替代目标和KL正则化结构；Hard-Pair-GRPO在此基础上引入显式局部概率约束和约束KL拟合优化，进一步抑制梯度噪声和全局策略漂移。

Result: 在HH-RLHF、UltraFeedback和MuJoCo HalfCheetah-v4上，Pair-GRPO系列在对齐质量、人类偏好胜率、训练稳定性和泛化能力上均优于现有基线；消融实验验证了各核心组件的关键贡献。

Conclusion: Pair-GRPO系列从理论上证明了单调策略改进、确定性梯度方向、梯度方差降低和动态步长收敛等保证，为偏好强化学习提供了统一且稳定的优化框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) alignment via reinforcement learning from human preferences (RLHF) suffers from unstable policy updates, ambiguous gradient directions, poor interpretability, and high gradient variance in mainstream pairwise preference learning paradigms. To systematically address these limitations, we establish a unified theoretical framework for preference-based RL optimization centered on the Pair-GRPO family, comprising two tightly coupled variants: Soft-Pair-GRPO and Hard-Pair-GRPO. Soft-Pair-GRPO is a minimal modification of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that replaces group-normalized scalar rewards with binary pairwise preference rewards, retaining GRPO's clipped surrogate and KL-regularized structure. We prove a critical gradient equivalence theorem: under first-order Taylor expansion around the current policy, Soft-Pair-GRPO's gradient is a positive scalar multiple of standard GRPO's gradient, explaining its empirical stability despite discarding continuous reward magnitudes. Building on this foundation, we propose Hard-Pair-GRPO, an advanced variant introducing explicit local probability constraints and constrained KL-fitting optimization to further suppress gradient noise and global policy drift. We provide comprehensive theoretical guarantees for both variants--including monotonic policy improvement, deterministic gradient direction, gradient-variance reduction, and dynamic step-size convergence. Extensive experiments on standard LLM alignment benchmarks (HH-RLHF,UltraFeedback) and the MuJoCo continuous control task HalfCheetah-v4 demonstrate that our Pair-GRPO family consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in alignment quality, human preference win rate, training stability, and generalization to general reinforcement learning. Ablation studies validate the critical contributions of each core component.

</details>


### [260] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现冻结预训练权重会削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出分层欺骗策略SSC来应对这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要关注从头训练场景，但在广泛使用的预训练-微调范式下的行为尚未被探索，存在关键空白。

Method: 提出分层欺骗策略SSC，将生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: 实验表明SSC在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下仍能保持数据不可学习性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键空白，验证了UE在PF范式下的行为差异并提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [261] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN提出了一种基于分布鲁棒优化的对抗训练框架，通过f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，在保持模型效用的同时显著降低攻击成功率，计算成本与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗训练方法计算昂贵且难以扩展，连续对抗训练虽提高了效率，但仍需更鲁棒的对抗样本重加权策略来提升防御效果。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架，通过f-散度（如KL散度）构建围绕经验训练分布的模糊集，优化最坏情况下的对抗损失，利用对偶形式转化为log-sum-exp目标函数，动态控制重加权强度。

Result: 在多种LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，计算和效用成本与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，通过信息论目标函数实现了高效对抗训练。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [262] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在f-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD进行了紧致分析，推导出可解释的闭式边界，并通过Berry-Esseen定理得到了紧致常数因子边界。研究表明在单轮次（E=1）下，当σ=1时，约需M≈1.14×10^6轮和N≈1.14×10^7训练样本即可实现有意义的差分隐私。多轮次组合导致δ具有线性依赖关系（E=O(√M)）。为突破Berry-Esseen限制，还提出了基于大数定律推广的新证明技术，实现了渐近随机猜测对角线极限结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有f-DP分析主要针对泊松子采样，其公式非封闭隐式、不透明且难以解释。随机洗牌子采样虽然实际中更常用，但缺乏紧致分析。同时，近期负结果表明在σ ≤ 1/√(2 ln M)区域存在局限，需要探索更优的参数区域。

Method: 采用f-DP框架分析随机洗牌子采样下的DP-SGD；应用Berry-Esseen定理推导紧致常数因子闭式边界；提出基于大数定律推广的新证明技术处理多轮次组合场景。

Result: 得到了随机洗牌子采样下DP-SGD的紧致闭式边界，比泊松子采样的非封闭隐式公式更具可解释性。对于E=1、σ=1、δ=1/100，约需M≈1.14×10^6和N≈1.14×10^7。多轮次组合下δ具有线性于E的依赖（E=O(√M)）。渐近分析表明当E=c_M²M且c_M→0时，组合后趋近随机猜测对角线，δ仅O(√E)依赖。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌子采样在f-DP框架下可获得比泊松子采样更优的透明闭式边界；存在一个可行的参数区域（σ ≥ √(3/ln M)）可克服近期负结果；多轮次组合限制E=O(√M)；渐近极限结果为开放问题留下空间。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [263] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero提出了一种PAC私有的零阶机制族，用于微调大语言模型，在互信息$I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$的严格隐私约束下实现可用效用。核心思想是通过符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度，在一致更新方向上实现零条件互信息的释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私（DP）框架在高隐私区域（$\varepsilon<1$）会产生过大噪声导致模型效用丧失，而PAC隐私通过条件互信息度量能在$I=0$的极端隐私预算下仍保持可用效用，填补了MIA（成员推断攻击）抵抗与模型效用之间的空白。

Method: 提出两种变体：PACZero-MI（通过精确校准二值释放来预算互信息）和PACZero-ZPL（在分歧步骤使用均匀掷硬币实现$I=0$）。核心创新点是对子集聚合的零阶梯度进行符号量化，当所有候选子集在更新方向上达成一致时，释放的符号花费零条件互信息。

Result: 在SST-2数据集上使用OPT-1.3B全参数微调，$I=0$时PACZero-ZPL达到88.99%准确率，仅比非私有的MeZO基线（91.1%）低2.1个百分点。在OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B上均获得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: PACZero在高隐私区域（$\varepsilon<1$或$I=0$）首次实现了可用效用，优于传统DP框架在该区域的性能表现，为隐私保护下的LLM微调提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [264] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAD是一个结合了聚类联邦学习（CFL）和双模式微架构（DM²A）的物联网入侵检测框架，能够在80%客户端无标签的情况下，以一半通信成本实现比现有方法高30%的检测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于联邦学习的入侵检测系统难以泛化到异构设备行为，且无法利用边缘环境中大量未标记数据，存在设备异构性和标签稀缺两大瓶颈。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，核心包含两个组件：1) 双模式微架构（DM²A），具有共享编码器和双分支结构，同时支持无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类；2) 动态聚类组件，将流量模式一致的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签场景下，检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本降低50%，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: CLAD通过整合聚类联邦学习和双模式架构，有效解决了物联网环境中设备异构和标签稀缺问题，实现了数据利用率最大化和通信效率优化。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [265] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非短视路径策略梯度（NM-PPG）方法，通过连续松弛和直通式 rollout 方案实现端到端优化非短视特征获取策略，在合成和真实数据集上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取（AFA）中特征获取成本高，现有方法多为短视策略或存在高方差问题，需要一种能够进行非短视决策且方差更低的端到端优化方法。

Method: 将 AFA 建模为 POMDP；引入获取过程的连续松弛以实现路径梯度；提出直通式 rollout 方案（前向硬获取、反向软传播）；结合熵正则化和温度锐化稳定优化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，NM-PPG 的性能优于现有最先进的 AFA 基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG 通过非短视路径策略梯度和直通式 rollout 有效解决了 AFA 中的特征获取优化问题，为低成本高精度预测提供了新方案。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [266] [Bayesian Rain Field Reconstruction using Commercial Microwave Links and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05520)
*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将微波链路（CML）降雨场重建视为贝叶斯逆问题，以扩散模型作为高保真空间先验，通过无训练的后验采样方法（Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛、副本交换）在合成和真实数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将CML简化为点传感器，忽略信号衰减与降雨之间的线积分关系，导致在非均匀降水条件下重建性能下降。

Method: 将降雨场重建建模为以扩散模型为先验的贝叶斯逆问题，利用Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛和副本交换等多种无训练后验采样方法进行求解。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法相比已建立的CML重建基线取得了一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 扩散模型作为先验能比截断高斯过程更好地保留关键降雨统计特征，为CML降雨场重建提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有有界支撑不可知噪声的线性估值情境动态定价问题，提出了Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，匹配了已知下界，改进了先前$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性估值情境动态定价算法依赖跨上下文插值论证，但不可知噪声导致的非Lipschitz需求曲线（含任意跳跃和原子）破坏了该论证基础，先前最佳方法仅实现$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾，与已知下界存在差距。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，该多项式时间算法结合了随机参数估计、保守残差网格探测和基于置信度的单步重定向。

Result: 算法实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，匹配了Kleinberg和Leighton (2003)的已知下界（对数因子内），并改进了Xu和Wang (2022)的上界。

Conclusion: 在随机良条件上下文下，该工作闭合了线性估值情境定价在不可知非Lipschitz噪声分布下长期存在的遗憾缺口。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [268] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DropsToGrid是一种基于神经过程的方法，通过融合不规则分布的气象站时间序列和雷达空间上下文，生成高分辨率、连续且带有不确定性量化的降雨场估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降雨观测存在偏差大、分辨率低的问题，现有深度学习密集化方法受限于降雨数据的偏态分布、局部性强、噪声大以及时空融合能力不足，亟需一种能有效融合稀疏地表观测与雷达数据的高分辨率降雨估计方法。

Method: 采用神经过程框架，结合多尺度特征提取、时间注意力机制和多模态融合模块，融合来自噪声、不规则分布的私有气象站的时间序列与雷达空间上下文信息，生成随机连续的降雨场估计并显式量化不确定性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DropsToGrid在仅有少量气象站可用和跨区域场景下，均优于运行基线和深度学习基线，生成准确的高分辨率降雨图且不确定性校准良好。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid通过有效融合多模态数据并量化不确定性，显著提升了高分辨率降雨估计的性能，验证了神经过程在稀疏到密集的降雨场重建中的有效性。

Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

</details>


### [269] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对核函数 bandit 优化中的误指定问题，提出通过谱局部化和空间局部化（域分割）机制，将误指定惩罚从传统的 $\sqrt{d_{\text{eff}}}$ 或 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 倍降低到对数或多项式对数增长水平。离线场景下，误指定项由谱 Lebesgue 常数控制；在线场景下，通过改进的域分割算法实现了 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\gamma_n n} + n\varepsilon)$ 的累积遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核函数 bandit 优化的理论保证中，误指定水平的惩罚依赖于核复杂度（有效维度 $d_{\text{eff}}$ 或最大信息增益 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$），导致惩罚因子过大。本文旨在降低这种误指定放大效应，使其仅产生对数级别的增长。

Method: 1) 离线场景：通过谱 Lebesgue 常数控制近似算子的误指定放大，针对一维多调谱和多元 Fourier 对角乘积核分别证明了对数和多项式对数放大。2) 在线场景：修改域分割算法，结合局部化特征值衰减假设，将误指定项从 $\sqrt{\gamma_n} n\varepsilon$ 降低到 $n\varepsilon$，去除了额外的 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子。

Result: 离线场景下得到高概率简单遗憾界，误指定项增长为对数级（一维谱）或多项式对数级（多元乘积核）。在线场景下得到累积遗憾界 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\gamma_n n} + n\varepsilon)$，较现有工作减少了 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 倍的误指定惩罚。

Conclusion: 谱局部化和空间局部化（域分割）是控制误指定放大的统一原则，能够显著降低核方法中误指定代价的理论上界。

Abstract: Existing guarantees for misspecified kernelized bandit optimization pay for misspecification through kernel complexity: in generic offline bounds, the misspecification level $\varepsilon$ is multiplied by $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$, where $d_\mathrm{eff}$ is the kernel effective dimension, while in online regret bounds, the corresponding penalty is $\sqrt{γ_n}\,n\varepsilon$, where $γ_n$ is the maximum information gain after $n$ rounds of interaction.
  In this work, we show that, for a large class of kernels, the misspecification amplification can be reduced to logarithmic or polylogarithmic growth. In the offline setting, we first prove high-probability simple-regret bounds whose misspecification term is governed by a spectral Lebesgue constant. This yields logarithmic amplification for one-dimensional monotone spectra and polylogarithmic amplification for multivariate Fourier-diagonal product kernels. In the online setting, we modify a domain-splitting algorithm and prove a cumulative regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ under mild localized eigendecay assumptions, removing the extra $\sqrt{γ_n}$ factor from the misspecification term. The common principle is localization: spectral localization controls the Lebesgue constant of the offline approximation operator, while domain splitting implements the spatial analogue of this mechanism in the online setting, preventing local misspecification errors from being amplified globally.

</details>


### [270] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoExVQA提出了一种自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计将证据识别、答案区域定位和答案解码分开，使推理过程透明可验证。在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，ANLS提升12%，达到可解释DocVQA的最新水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DocVQA模型将问题相关证据和答案定位纠缠在一起，本质上是黑盒模型，无法验证预测如何依赖于视觉证据。

Method: CoExVQA采用链式解释设计：先识别问题相关证据，再显式定位答案区域，最后仅从定位区域解码答案，使推理过程可检查和验证。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，ANLS比当前可解释基线提升12%，达到可解释DocVQA的最优性能。

Conclusion: 将解码限制在地面证据上能够实现透明且可验证的预测，同时保持高性能。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [271] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出元博弈（metagame）框架，通过将归因方法本身视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化模型解释的二阶交互效应，从理论上证明归因可分层分解为元归因，并在语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和多模态扩散模型中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法主要关注一阶特征归因，但缺乏对特征间二阶交互效应（即一个特征如何影响另一个特征归因）的系统量化框架。

Method: 将归因方法视为合作博弈，通过计算其Shapley值定义从特征j到特征i的元归因φ_{j→i}(f)；理论上建立归因到元归因的分层分解，并将其与现有交互指标建立方向性扩展关系。

Result: 在三种可解释性应用中展示有效性：(i) 量化指令微调语言模型中的token间交互，(ii) 解释视觉-语言编码器的跨模态相似性，(iii) 解释多模态扩散Transformer中文本到图像的概念。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架为理解模型解释中的二阶交互效应提供了理论严谨且实践有效的方法，拓展了可解释性分析的维度。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [272] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次感知交叉熵（HACE），通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑将类别层次结构融入损失函数，在CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft和NABirds三个数据集上，端到端训练中15/18架构-数据集对提升准确率（平均+4.66%），线性探测中全面超越基线（平均+2.18%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵平等对待所有 misclassifications，忽略了类别层次结构中蕴含的语义距离信息，无法利用细粒度分类中的层级关系。

Method: 提出HACE损失函数，包含两个组件：(1) 预测聚合：将模型概率质量沿层次结构向上传播，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；(2) 祖先标签平滑：将真实标签信号沿从真类到根节点的路径进行分配。

Result: 端到端训练中，HACE在18个架构-数据集对中有15个超越标准交叉熵，平均提升4.66%；线性探测中，在三个数据集上均优于所有对比方法，平均比次优基线提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的即插即用替代方案，能有效利用类别层次结构提升分类性能，在卷积和注意力架构上均表现稳定。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [273] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究迭代随机净化防御的白盒攻击鲁棒评估，提出利用梯度检查点技术实现长轨迹上的精确端到端梯度计算，构建内存高效的全梯度评估框架，发现全梯度攻击能揭示近似梯度遗漏的漏洞，证明精确梯度评估对可靠基准测试至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代随机净化防御的评估因内存限制常采用近似反向传播，这会削弱攻击信号并高估鲁棒性；同时，迭代过程中的随机性未被充分控制，不同净化轨迹会显著改变鲁棒性指标。

Method: 提出结合梯度检查点反向传播与随机性控制评估协议的内存高效全梯度评估框架，在扩散模型和基于能量模型（EBM）的朗之万采样上验证。

Result: 全梯度攻击发现了近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞，在ℓ∞和ℓ₂白盒攻击上取得当前最优结果，并能探测分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估是可靠基准测试迭代随机防御的必要条件。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [274] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视顺序无关自回归模型（OA-ARM）在缺失数据场景下的应用，提出缺失感知的MO-ARM模型，证明其标准训练隐含MCAR假设下的隐式插补能力，并首次建立非随机缺失机制下的训练框架，同时利用其条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，在多个基准上超越传统插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但在不完整数据场景下的应用尚属空白。现有插补方法在高度缺失或非随机缺失机制下性能有限，亟需一种能够处理各种缺失机制的统一框架。

Method: 1) 重新解释OA-ARM的标准训练过程，证明其在完全观测数据上隐式执行MCAR机制下的插补；2) 提出首个可直接在不完整数据上训练OA-ARM的理论框架，支持一般性缺失机制；3) 利用模型的摊销条件密度估计能力，主动选择最具信息量的缺失变量以优化下游任务。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，MO-ARM在插补准确性和下游预测任务中一致优于传统插补基线方法。

Conclusion: 顺序无关自回归模型可作为缺失数据处理的有效框架，其隐式插补性质和在非随机缺失下的可训练性为实际应用中常见的不完整数据问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [275] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测MoE模型是否会出现"死专家"现象。通过12个控制实验（8视觉+4语言）共11000+训练epoch验证，发现E ≥ 0.5即可保证零死专家，无需人工设计的负载均衡辅助损失。此外还发现了死专家可复苏、正交毒性具有数据集依赖性等6个额外发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型(MoE)训练中经常出现"死专家"现象（路由从不选择某些专家），传统解决方案依赖手动的负载均衡辅助损失函数，缺乏统一的定量预测指标。作者希望找到一个类似流体力学中雷诺数的统一诊断参数，来预测和控制MoE训练的生态健康度。

Method: 定义控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，其中T为路由温度，H为路由熵权重，O为Oracle权重，B为平衡权重。通过12个控制实验（CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2/103等数据集），系统性地改变E值来观察专家生态健康度，验证E ≥ 0.5作为零死专家阈值的有效性。

Result: E ≥ 0.5即可保证零死专家，无需人工设计负载均衡损失。6个额外发现：死专家可被平衡损失驱动的路由重探索所复苏；正交毒性具有数据集依赖性；任务复杂度会改变临界E阈值；模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；三层MoE会自发坍缩为两层功能结构；生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E参数可作为MoE训练的 unified diagnostic，其地位类似于流体力学中的雷诺数。通过调整E值可以可靠地避免死专家问题，简化了MoE训练的复杂性。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [276] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR框架，通过矩匹配学习数据权重，实现离线策略评估，在仅需$Q^\pi$可实现性条件下获得维度无关的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法在函数近似下缺乏数据相关的有限样本保证，或保证依赖于函数类的统计复杂度（如VC维），且对覆盖条件（coverage）的理解不够深入。

Method: Q-MMR通过矩匹配目标函数，以自顶向下的归纳方式学习每个数据点的标量权重，使得加权后的奖励近似目标策略下的期望回报。权重学习依赖于值函数判别器类。

Result: 在仅需$Q^\pi$可实现性的条件下，建立了维度无关（不依赖于函数类统计复杂度）的数据相关有限样本保证。并与重要性采样、线性FQE等方法建立了联系。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离线策略评估提供了新的理论视角，揭示了覆盖条件的本质，具有更宽松的理论假设和更优的有限样本保证。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [277] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲概念瓶颈模型(HypCBM)，通过将概念嵌入双曲空间并利用蕴含锥的几何包含关系，实现层次结构感知的稀疏概念激活，无需额外监督即可在低数据场景下达到或超越传统欧氏模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型(CBM)将概念嵌入平坦欧氏空间，视其为独立正交维度，忽略了概念天然具有的语义层次结构，导致模型无法捕捉概念间的包含、继承等层级关系。

Method: 提出HypCBM后验框架：1) 将概念激活重新建模为双曲空间中蕴含锥内的不对称几何包含关系；2) 利用蕴含锥内的包含边界作为测试时激活信号，无需额外监督或学习模块即可得到稀疏、层次感知的激活；3) 引入自适应缩放法则，实现层次保真的人为干预传播。

Result: HypCBM在稀疏设定下（人类可解释性所需）与使用20倍数据训练的欧氏后验模型性能相当，具有更强的层次一致性和对输入损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将概念瓶颈从欧氏空间迁移至双曲空间，利用几何结构编码概念层次关系，是一种高效且无需额外标注的改进方向，兼具可解释性与实用性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [278] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MELO方法，通过多尺度遗忘因子加权的指数加权最小二乘在线聚合，无需先验知识即可自适应不同时间尺度的分布漂移，在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID封锁）中降低RMSE 34.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中存在分布漂移，预测器需要在平稳期保持稳定、在漂移期快速适应，但最优适应记忆长度未知，现有方法无法自适应处理不同时间尺度的漂移。

Method: MELO：将任意非前瞻基础预测器池与多遗忘因子的EWLS自适应专家结合，使用无参数在线聚合规则MLpol聚合原始预测和EWLS自适应预测，实现轻量级递归更新。

Result: 在法国电力负荷预测（COVID封锁期间）中，无需任何漂移指标或政策协变量，MELO相对纯MLpol降低RMSE 34.7%，且优于配备外部COVID政策协变量的TabICL基线。

Conclusion: MELO是一种轻量、模型无关的在线聚合方法，能在分布漂移下自适应最优遗忘尺度，具备确定的oracle不等式保证，在实际电力负荷预测中显著优于基线。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [279] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基础模型的分布外(OOD)问题在结构上不同于传统OOD，提出了一个分阶段感知的形式化框架，证明了模型中心方法存在参数覆盖上限，并主张智能体系统作为解决该问题的必要补充范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临的知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放式任务变化等OOD现象，其本质不同于传统OOD研究设定，且预训练和后训练分布通常仅被部分观测，现有模型中心范式无法有效应对。

Method: 通过四步论证：(1)提出分阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，适应部分观测的多阶段训练分布；(2)证明参数覆盖上限定理，指出存在模型中心方法无法处理的输入；(3)刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性：感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证，并证明其严格超越覆盖上限；(4)回应七个反驳论点，承认其中两个。

Result: 证明了模型中心方法存在固有的参数覆盖上限，而具备感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证的智能体系统能严格扩展可处理输入的范围；发现模型中心与智能体方法互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD是结构上独特的问题，需要将智能体范式作为独立的研究方向来推进，与模型中心方法互补。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [280] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带softmax注意力的Transformer如何在线性分类数据上进行上下文学习，构建了能执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，其中每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，并通过单层自注意力的训练和循环应用获得该模型，同时提供了训练收敛和泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer为何能通过上下文学习隐式执行算法，从而增强预测和生成能力。

Method: 构造多层Transformer实现上下文逻辑回归，每层对应一步归一化梯度下降；通过单层自注意力训练（由一步梯度下降监督）再循环应用得到该模型；并提供训练收敛性和分布外泛化保证。

Result: 构造的Transformer每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降；训练收敛保证和分布外泛化保证均已证明。

Conclusion: Softmax Transformer可以有效地作为上下文学习器，加深了我们对上下文学习机制的理论理解。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [281] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文澄清了TurboQuant与更早的DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021)和EDEN (ICML 2022)方法之间的关系，指出TurboQuant是EDEN的特例且存在多处次优性，实验表明EDEN在精度上全面优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TurboQuant与EDEN/DRIVE之间的关系，纠正TurboQuant声称的新颖性误解，并展示EDEN在量化精度上的优越性。

Method: 通过理论分析指出：(1) TurboQuant_mse是EDEN在固定标量尺度S=1时的特例，该选择通常次优；(2) TurboQuant_prod存在三层次优性：使用次优S=1、1-bit无偏残差量化MSE劣于EDEN、级联方式劣于直接用b-bit EDEN无偏量化；(3) TurboQuant的分析方法与EDEN存在相似性。实验上在TurboQuant的所有精度实验中比较EDEN的性能。

Result: 有偏EDEN（优化S）精度优于TurboQuant_mse；无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod，常超出1比特优势（如2-bit EDEN优于3-bit TurboQuant_prod）；在所有TurboQuant报告的精度实验中，EDEN均表现更优。

Conclusion: TurboQuant并未超越EDEN，反而是EDEN的更次优特例；EDEN在所有设置下均优于TurboQuant。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [282] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出了一种动态Top-K选择机制，使稀疏自编码器能够根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，克服了传统TopK SAE固定稀疏度无法匹配数据内在可变维度的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据流形的局部本征维度是变化的，固定稀疏度（固定K值）无法适应不同输入的复杂度差异——简单输入可能因过多激活特征引入噪声，复杂输入则可能因激活特征不足丢失重要结构信息。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，采用可微分的Soft Top-K算子替代传统的硬Top-K选择，使模型能够学习输入依赖的稀疏度k，从而根据每个输入的复杂度动态调整激活特征的数目。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能够发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择恰当数量的特征进行表征。

Conclusion: 动态稀疏度比固定稀疏度更契合数据的流形结构，解释性长度能自然反映输入的信息量，为机械可解释性中特征分解的自适应稀疏化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [283] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中注意力集中现象（attention sink）的机制根源：自注意力中的值聚合过程导致系统性方差差异，且FFN层中超级神经元的激活会显著放大这一差异，从而迫使第一个token形成注意力集中作为结构锚点。通过两种干预手段可在任意位置复现该现象，并提出了head-wise RMSNorm架构修改来稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已发现LLM中存在注意力集中现象（初始token垄断注意力分数），但其结构性起源尚不明确。本文旨在从机制层面解释该现象的成因。

Method: 1. 理论分析：追溯注意力集中根源于自注意力中的值聚合过程导致的方差差异，并发现FFN层中超级神经元的激活会放大这一差异；2. 干预实验：通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应，以及通过放大目标token表示的方差，在任意位置复现注意力集中；3. 提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改，在预训练过程中稳定值聚合输出。

Result: 两种干预方法均能在任意位置成功复现注意力集中现象；head-wise RMSNorm通过恢复不同位置间的统计对等性，显著加速了模型收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了注意力集中现象的机制性解释，为系统性地控制该现象提供了基础，并通过head-wise RMSNorm架构修改证明了恢复位置间统计对等性能有效加速预训练收敛。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [284] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线贝叶斯校准框架BRPC，通过分离粒子更新与条件高斯过程解决参数-偏差混杂问题，并集成重启机制应对系统突变，在流式数据场景下提升校准精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-偏差混杂问题且仅适用于离线静态场景，无法满足数字孪生中系统随时间演化的需求（渐变漂移与突变切换）。现有数据同化方法虽支持在线更新，但未显式建模系统偏差，在突变场景下效果不佳。

Method: 提出BRPC框架：1) 将投影校准扩展至在线设置，通过分离无偏差粒子更新（校准参数）与条件高斯过程更新（偏差函数）保持可辨识性；2) 集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程；3) 提供渐变跟踪性能与重启检测行为（虚警/检测率）的理论保证。

Result: 在合成与植物仿真基准上，BRPC在渐变场景下提高了校准精度；重启增强的BRPC在突变场景下相比滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线，鲁棒性与预测性能显著提升。

Conclusion: BRPC框架有效解决了在线贝叶斯校准中的偏差建模与非平稳性挑战，为数字孪生中的连续系统校准提供了理论基础与实践方案。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [285] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了混沌系统与预测多重性之间的理论联系，通过引入时域约束的Rashomon集框架，揭示了混沌系统中模型多样性随预测时域指数级收缩的规律，并基于此开发了决策对齐的模型选择算法，在合成系统与真实应用中实现了18-34%的决策质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（存在多个表现相近但结构不同的模型）与混沌动力学（初始微小差异导致指数级发散）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基础挑战，尽管它们在概念上存在深层联系——混沌系统的内在不稳定性会放大模型间的差异，但尚无理论框架统一这两者。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出时域约束Rashomon集理论框架，证明混沌系统中有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率随预测提前时间指数收缩；引入Lyapunov加权的度量指标，为预测不一致性提供更紧的界；开发决策对齐的选择算法，根据下游效用而非单纯预测精度从近最优模型中选型。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，所提框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升了18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间首个严谨的理论联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了有理论依据的指导原则。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [286] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了概念引导（concept steering）的理论框架，建立了引导与线性概念擦除（LEACE）之间的联系，提出了LEACE-Switch用于概念切换，并引入MidSteer作为更通用的最小扰动引导方法，在多种模型和任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管引导中间表示（steering intermediate representations）在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全控制中经验性成功，但目前缺乏一个全面的理论框架来支撑。

Method: 首先建立引导与概念擦除的联系，证明标准引导方法是LEACE的特例；然后形式化概念切换框架LEACE-Switch并刻画其最优性条件；最后提出MidSteer，一种更通用的仿射框架，放松了假设条件，支持定向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）中均取得有利表现。

Conclusion: 本文为概念引导提供了系统的理论支撑，MidSteer作为通用框架能有效实现定向最小扰动引导，具有良好的跨模态、跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [287] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，通过轻量级门控机制让每个token自适应决定跳过某些Transformer层，在不修改基础架构且仅增加1.7%参数的情况下，实现端到端可微的差异化计算路由，在字符级语言建模中节省14-23%的计算量且质量损失<0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同数量的层，忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算资源的浪费。

Method: 设计一个轻量级两层MLP作为门控，产生连续的停等概率，控制每个token在相邻Transformer块之间的残差更新是否被保留，实现端到端可微的差异化计算路由。

Result: 在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上，以<0.5%的质量损失节省14-23%的token-layer操作；在相同效率下验证损失比early exit低0.7%；学习的路由可直接用于推理时的稀疏执行以获得实际加速。

Conclusion: TSA能学习到与难度成比例的路由策略，即使在没有深度正则化的情况下，任务损失梯度本身就能驱动路由器跳过约20%的token-layer操作，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [288] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏自编码器在变压器架构中特征组合操控的稳定性问题，提出了一个几何分析框架，揭示了在高维稀疏锥流形下特征联合存在渐近性组合坍塌阈值，并证明ReLU整流会导致类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，最终在CLEVR数据集上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽能有效解耦特征叠加并实现激活操控，但组合式操控（同时激活多个语义潜变量）的理论基础尚不明确，线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中存在的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下利用高斯均值宽度（统计维度）推导组合坍塌的渐近阈值；分析高偏置下ReLU整流如何将微观相关性诱导的方差波动转化为系统漂移，形成类似棘轮效应的干扰增长。

Result: 推导出组合操控的坍塌阈值，揭示了ReLU整流导致的干扰累积棘轮效应，并在CLEVR数据集上验证了层级相关性会加速坍塌转变的理论预测。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于联合的操控可扩展性受几何约束限制，需要设计能显式管理干扰的组合机制，而非依赖朴素线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [289] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS是一个无需训练的高效推理框架，通过熵加权负载和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中存在严重的尾延迟效应，而现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决两个关键挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源分配不均。

Method: 提出MACS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制——量化视觉token的语义价值，解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制——根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源。该方法无需训练，直接应用于推理阶段。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上，MACS显著优于现有方法，为MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了稳健的解决方案。

Conclusion: MACS通过无需训练的轻量级框架有效解决了MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈，验证了模态感知的容量缩放方法的有效性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [290] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种监督内化方法，使模型能够在仅使用结果级监督的条件下，通过识别、纠正和复用失败的推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，从而实现更细粒度的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
