Motivation: 现有强化学习方法要么依赖结果级奖励导致信用分配困难，要么依赖外部构建的过程监督成本高昂且难以规模化扩展，亟需一种能在仅结果监督下实现细粒度信用分配的方法。

Method: 提出将"结果监督内化为过程监督"的新视角，通过让模型自动识别、纠正和复用失败的推理轨迹，从中提取过程级学习信号，实现更细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 所提方法能够在仅结果监督条件下实现细粒度信用分配，避免了外部过程监督的高昂成本，为推理强化学习开辟了新路径。

Conclusion: 强化学习中的推理问题本质上可以理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的过程，所提出的监督内化范式为细粒度信用分配提供了一种不同于外部过程监督的新方案。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [291] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，将数据整理从离线选择/混合转变为在线动态重加权，通过基于相似度的自适应学习率在训练过程中动态调整样本重要性，无需改变样本数量，在指令微调和预训练中均优于传统离线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据整理方法（如数据选择、混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、在模型或任务变化时需要重新运行整个流程，且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模会牺牲多样性，损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT（Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning），一种动态在线框架：在训练过程中通过基于相似度的质量信号为每个样本分配自适应学习率，以损失加权方式动态调整样本重要性，保持样本数量不变，实现隐式课程学习。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验上，ADAPT在同等FLOPs下一致优于离线数据选择/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将数据整理视为在线重加权问题比传统离线范式更有效，ADAPT通过动态调整样本重要性实现了隐式课程学习，提升了LLM训练的效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [292] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于进化策略的量化后优化方法，通过迭代调整少量权重的量化状态，在保持模型压缩效果的同时提升量化模型的准确率，并在VGG、ResNet和自编码器架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在物联网、移动设备等资源受限场景下应用受限，其复杂度和内存大小是主要障碍。现有量化技术多基于最近邻取整，但该方式无法保证最优精度，因此需要更高效的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，在预训练并量化后的模型中，每次迭代仅改变少量权重的值，使其转移到不同的量化状态。通过设计合适的操作子和参数集，迭代优化模型的最终精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet（图像分类与检测）以及自编码器架构上进行了实验，结果表明所提进化策略能够快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于最近邻取整的量化状态并非最优，通过进化策略对量化后的权重进行微调可以有效提升模型精度，该方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [293] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CopyCop算法，用于检测一个GNN是否作为“复制猫”模仿另一个GNN的输出嵌入，即使两者架构、权重、嵌入维度不同，且对手可能对输出进行变换。实验证明该方法准确且鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印和指纹方法无法在GNN架构、权重、嵌入维度不同且对手可能进行变换的条件下，有效识别复制猫GNN。需要一种更通用的所有权验证方法。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，能够在两个GNN输出嵌入之间建立关联性检测，判定是否存在复制关系，并提供理论保证。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop能够准确识别复制猫GNN，并且在面对多种对抗攻击和变换时保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CopyCop是一种有效且鲁棒的GNN所有权验证方法，能在严苛条件下识别复制猫模型，补充了现有水印和指纹方法的不足。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [294] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法中位数提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞品快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（<5%），且需要针对全新蛋白质从头开始迭代筛选，亟需高效的配体选择方法以减少测试次数。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，采用新型配体选择策略，在稀疏数据下学习并迭代筛选候选配体。

Result: 平均40次测试找到10个高质量配体；一对一比较中，在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞品快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为针对新型蛋白质的药物发现提供了高效的配体筛选方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [295] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，通过将路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两阶段，结合预编码边聚合、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，有效解决多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性问题，在六个VRP变体上达到或超越SOTA性能，同时大幅提升训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图场景（平行边代表不同旅行选项）缺乏高效可扩展的解决方案，已有方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 1) 节点-边策略分解（NEPF），将路由策略拆分为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；2) 预编码边聚合方案，支持两阶段分解；3) 非自回归架构用于边选择阶段；4) 分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上，NEPF在解质量上匹配或超越SOTA，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF方法有效解决了多图VRP的可扩展性问题，为处理带平行边的复杂路由场景提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [296] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理会导致模型崩溃（始终预测"是"或"否"）。本文提出基于图的语义损失函数和动态λ调度，在Gemma 270M上使传递性任务达到70.4%准确率、d-分离任务达到68.6%准确率，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理任务会导致灾难性的模型崩溃，模型学习到始终预测"是"或"否"的平凡解，无法进行真正的因果推理。

Method: 提出一种包含图结构逻辑约束和动态λ调度的语义损失函数，替代标准微调中的交叉熵损失，防止模型学习到与输入结构无关的平凡解。

Result: 在Gemma 270M上，传递性任务准确率70.4%，d-分离任务准确率68.6%，较崩溃基线提升42.7%。在1000个对抗样本上，语义模型达67-70%准确率，崩溃模型仅43-71%。在20万+评估样本上验证了五组模型变体。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer进行稳定因果推理是必要且不可选的，标准微调必然导致模型崩溃，而引入图结构逻辑约束的语义损失能有效防止这一现象。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [297] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次全面评估了图自监督学习（GSSL）在文本驱动噪声图上的表现，提出了NATD-GSSL统一框架，包含自动图构建、图精炼和GSSL。通过双图协议对比MedMentions噪声图与UMLS清洁图，发现不同预任务和GNN架构对噪声的鲁棒性差异显著：关系重构对噪声敏感但受益于良好模式，特征重构最鲁棒，对比学习受噪声影响较小但依赖下游任务对齐。双向关系消息传递架构更适合噪声图，最高可比预训练语言模型提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSSL研究假设图是干净、人工整理的，但NLP的进步使得可以从文本自动大规模提取知识图谱，这带来了真实世界的噪声。此前鲁棒性研究仅依赖合成扰动，缺乏对文本驱动图中真实噪声影响的探索。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL框架，包含三个模块：自动图构建（从文本提取知识图谱）、图精炼、GSSL。采用双图评估协议：噪声图来自MedMentions，清洁参考图来自UMLS，通过对齐共享金标准进行比较。系统评估多种预任务（关系重构、特征重构、对比学习）和GNN架构（双向关系消息传递、单向关系消息传递等）。

Result: 1）关系重构任务对噪声高度敏感，但良好定义的模式可缓解；2）特征重构鲁棒性最强，性能接近清洁图设置；3）对比学习受噪声影响较小，但高度依赖与下游任务的对齐；4）双向关系消息传递GNN更适合噪声文本驱动图，单向关系GNN在清洁图上表现更优；5）NATD-GSSL相比预训练语言模型基线最高提升7%。

Conclusion: 该研究为将GSSL应用于真实世界噪声图提供了实用指导，揭示了不同预任务和架构对文本驱动噪声的鲁棒性差异，强调了在自动构建的噪声图上选择合适GSSL组件的重要性。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [298] [MOSAIC: Module Discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal Learning for Scientific Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05524)
*Shicheng Fan,Nour Elhendawy,Jianle Sun,Ke Fang,Kun Zhang,Yihang Wang,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种稀疏时间VAE模型，通过将可观测变量的可解释性迁移到可识别的潜在空间，实现科学时间序列中潜在机制的模块级可解释发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习(CRL)的可识别性保证不意味着可解释性，潜在语义通常需要通过后验对齐来指定。这在科学时间序列中尤为突出，因为底层机制未知，而发现可解释结构是主要目标。相反，科学观测数据本身具有语义性（对应命名的物理量），因此提出能否将观测的可解释性迁移到可识别的潜在空间。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，集成：（1）基于状态条件时间变分的时间CRL可识别性；（2）通过加性解码器为每个潜在变量恢复稀疏的观测变量关联集合；（3）采用ANOVA主效应支撑的识别性保证，以及稀疏加性变体的有限样本恢复保证。

Result: 在RNA分子动力学、太阳风、ENSO气候、田纳西伊士曼过程及合成托卡马克基准等多个科学时间序列数据上，MOSAIC恢复了领域一致的变量分组，实现了潜在机制的可解释发现。

Conclusion: MOSAIC成功地将观测变量的可解释性（模块级语义）转移到可识别的潜在空间，在保证识别性的同时实现了科学时间序列中潜在机制的可解释性发现，弥补了CRL识别性与可解释性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to recover latent variables with identifiability guarantees, typically up to permutation and component-wise reparameterization under appropriate assumptions. However, identifiability does not imply interpretability: latent semantics are typically assigned post hoc by alignment with known ground-truth factors. This limitation is particularly acute in scientific time series, where underlying mechanisms are unknown and discovering interpretable structure is a primary goal. In contrast, scientific observations (such as residue-pair distances, climate indices, or process sensors) are inherently semantic, as they correspond to named physical quantities. This raises a key question: can the interpretability of observations be transferred to the identifiable latent space? We propose MOSAIC (Module discovery via Sparse Additive Identifiable Causal learning), a sparse temporal VAE that integrates temporal CRL identifiability with support recovery over observed variables. MOSAIC identifies latent variables via regime-conditioned temporal variation, and recovers for each latent a sparse set of associated observations through an additive decoder, yielding module-level interpretability. We show that ANOVA main-effect supports are identifiable under general smooth mixing functions, and provide finite-sample recovery guarantees for a tractable sparse-additive variant. Empirically, MOSAIC recovers domain-consistent variable groups across RNA molecular dynamics, solar wind, ENSO climate, the Tennessee Eastman process, and a synthetic tokamak benchmark, enabling interpretable discovery of latent mechanisms in scientific time series.

</details>


### [299] [Accelerating LMO-Based Optimization via Implicit Gradient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05577)
*Won-Jun Jang,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LMO-IGT方法，利用隐式梯度传输（IGT）加速基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的优化方法，在保持单梯度每迭代结构的同时实现更优的收敛性（O(ε^{-3.5})），并引入统一分析框架和新的平稳性度量（正则化支持函数RSF）。Muon-IGT实例在实验中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMO优化器（如Lion、Muon）经验表现强但理论碎片化；方差缩减虽可加速但计算开销大；需统一理论框架并设计高效加速方案。

Method: 提出LMO-IGT，利用隐式梯度传输在传输点评估随机梯度；引入正则化支持函数（RSF）作为统一平稳性度量；构建随机LMO优化统一框架。

Result: 随机LMO迭代复杂度O(ε^{-4})，方差缩减LMO为O(ε^{-3})（需额外梯度），LMO-IGT为O(ε^{-3.5})（仅单梯度）。实验上LMO-IGT持续优于基线，Muon-IGT综合表现最强。

Conclusion: 隐式梯度传输为现代LMO优化器提供了一种有效且实用的加速机制，理论分析与实验验证均支持其优势。

Abstract: Recent optimizers such as Lion and Muon have demonstrated strong empirical performance by normalizing gradient momentum via linear minimization oracles (LMOs). While variance reduction has been explored to accelerate LMO-based methods, it typically incurs substantial computational overhead due to additional gradient evaluations. At the same time, the theoretical understanding of LMO-based methods remains fragmented across unconstrained and constrained formulations. Motivated by these limitations, we propose \emph{LMO-IGT}, a new class of stochastic LMO-based methods leveraging implicit gradient transport (IGT). We further introduce a unified framework for stochastic LMO-based optimization together with a new stationarity measure, the \emph{regularized support function} (RSF), which bridges gradient-norm and Frank--Wolfe-gap notions within a common framework. By evaluating stochastic gradients at transported points, LMO-IGT accelerates convergence while retaining the single-gradient-per-iteration structure of standard stochastic LMO. Our analysis establishes that stochastic LMO achieves an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$, variance-reduced LMO achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ at the cost of additional gradient evaluations, and LMO-IGT achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3.5})$ using only a single stochastic gradient per iteration. Empirically, LMO-IGT consistently improves over stochastic LMO counterparts with negligible overhead. Among its instantiations, Muon-IGT achieves the strongest overall performance across evaluated settings, demonstrating that IGT provides an effective and practical acceleration mechanism for modern LMO-based optimization.

</details>


### [300] [Budgeted Attention Allocation: Cost-Conditioned Compute Control for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05697)
*Amrit Nidhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预算注意力分配机制，通过单调头门控在单个模型中实现不同注意力预算下的推理，可在准确率和计算成本间灵活权衡，并在多个任务上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署系统通常需要多个成本-质量运行点，但现有Transformer模型通常每个训练好的模型只能暴露一个推理成本，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出Budgeted Attention Allocation，一种基于请求注意力预算的单调头门控机制；采用稠密热启动保证稳定性；支持软成本控制到硬门控的结构性加速转换。

Result: 合成序列任务上达99.7%准确率@0.303成本和100.0%准确率@0.504成本；AG News上词级Transformer达82.1%准确率@1.28x加速；BERT-Mini达87.6%准确率@1.20x加速；DBpedia14上97.4% vs 96.6%基准。

Conclusion: 贡献不在于全面超越，而在于展示了一个可控检查点在多个预算间权衡注意力成本与准确率、并转化为实测结构性加速的可行复现研究。

Abstract: Transformers usually expose one inference cost per trained model, while deployed systems often need multiple cost-quality operating points. We study Budgeted Attention Allocation, a monotone head-gating mechanism conditioned on a requested attention budget. Dense warm-starting is important for stability: on a robust synthetic sequence task, one budgeted model reaches 99.7% accuracy at 0.303 estimated attention cost and 100.0% accuracy at 0.504 cost. On held-out AG News with a custom word-level transformer, hard-gate adaptation turns soft cost control into measured single-thread CPU speed, reaching 82.1% accuracy with 1.28x speedup at budget 0.50. In pretrained BERT-Mini AG News, budgeted structural pruning reaches 87.6% accuracy with 1.20x speedup at budget 0.50; a validation-ranked zero-shot dense post-hoc structural baseline reaches 86.1%, and one recovery epoch raises that per-budget specialist to 87.9%. On DBpedia14, BERT-Mini budgeted gates reach 97.4% at exact budget 0.50 versus 96.6% for dense full attention. Static fixed-budget gates and recovered dense specialists remain strong. The contribution is therefore not universal dominance, but a reproducible feasibility study of one controllable checkpoint across budgets that can trade attention cost for accuracy and be converted into measured structural speedups on small CPU benchmarks.

</details>


### [301] [CRAFT: Forgetting-Aware Intervention-Based Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05732)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Fatema Siddika,Juan Pablo Munoz,Tanya Roosta,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个持续学习框架，通过学习隐藏表示上的低秩干预来避免更新模型权重，利用基于KL散度的统一目标函数实现任务路由、正则化和合并，在多个基准和模型规模上优于LoRA方法，并能有效减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过微调可以获得新能力，但持续适应通常会导致灾难性遗忘。现有方法难以在不牺牲旧任务性能的情况下高效地学习新任务。

Method: CRAFT包含三个阶段：(1) 基于输出分布散度将每个任务路由到相似任务组；(2) 使用KL散度对组先验状态进行微调，直接控制遗忘并决定收敛；(3) 使用相同的KL信号将更新后的任务干预合并到共享表示中。整个框架通过单一的KL目标统一了路由、正则化和合并。

Result: 在多个基准和模型规模上，CRAFT相比强LoRA基线方法在整体性能上有所提升，且有效减少了遗忘，同时对任务顺序具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在表示空间中控制适应过程，并以输出空间散度为指导，为大语言模型的持续学习提供了一种可扩展且原则性的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can acquire new capabilities through fine-tuning, but continual adaptation often leads to catastrophic forgetting. We propose CRAFT, a continual learning framework that avoids updating model weights by instead learning low-rank interventions on hidden representations. CRAFT proceeds in three stages: it first routes each task to a group of similar tasks based on output-distribution divergence; it then fine-tunes the model using a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the group's prior state, which directly controls forgetting and determines convergence; finally, it merges interventions for the updated task into the shared representation using the same KL signal. This design unifies routing, regularization, and merging through a single KL-based objective. CRAFT improves overall performance and reduces forgetting compared to strong LoRA-based approaches across multiple benchmarks and model scales, while remaining robust to task ordering. These results suggest that controlling adaptation in representation space, guided by output-space divergence, provides a scalable and principled approach to continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [302] [Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05739)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种行为评估框架，通过LLM裁判对智能体股票预测系统的六个决策维度进行评分，并将维度级缺陷转化为惩罚项纳入SAC奖励函数，经三轮微调后在测试集上实现MAPE降低11.5%、方向准确率提升至74%、Sharp比率提升18%，改进主要集中在高波动时段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体股票预测系统涉及一系列相互依赖的决策（如态势检测、路径路由、强化学习控制），但现有评估仅依赖MAPE或方向准确率等聚合指标，无法揭示单个决策环节的质量缺陷，缺乏细粒度的行为级评估与反馈机制。

Method: 1) 在系统的每个自主决策点记录行为轨迹，以五日为跨度分组为episode；2) 由GPT 5.4、Claude 4.6 Opus、Gemini 3.1 Pro三个LLM裁判组成的集成裁判，沿态势检测、路由、适应、风险校准、策略一致性、错误恢复六个维度打分；3) 通过扰动验证（420个episode）确认维度特异性（目标维度得分下降-1.6至-2.4，其余五维仅平均下降-0.32，跨模型一致性Krippendorff's α=0.85）；4) 将维度级缺陷转化为信用分配的惩罚项，加入SAC奖励函数进行微调。

Result: 在2017-2025年测试集上：一日MAPE从0.61%降至0.54%（相对降低11.5%，p<0.001，Cohen's d=0.31）；方向准确率从71%提升至74%；Sharp比率提升18%（95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]）。改进主要集中高波动episode，复合行为评分与离线回测20日Sharp比率的相关性ρ=0.72。

Conclusion: 基于行为评估的多维度LLM裁判框架能够有效识别智能体股票预测系统中的细粒度决策缺陷，并通过将行为评分反馈到强化学习奖励机制中实现系统性改进。但结果仅来自离线回测，未涉及实盘部署中的具体影响。

Abstract: Agentic stock prediction systems make sequences of interdependent decisions (regime detection, pathway routing, reinforcement learning control) whose individual quality is hidden by aggregate metrics such as mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) or directional accuracy. We present a behavioral evaluation framework that addresses this gap. Behavioral traces logged at every autonomous decision point are grouped into five-day episodes and scored along six domain-specific dimensions (regime detection, routing, adaptation, risk calibration, strategy coherence, error recovery) by an ensemble of three large language model (LLM) judges (GPT 5.4, Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro). Perturbation-based validation on 420 episodes yields targeted score drops of $-1.6$ to $-2.4$ on intended dimensions versus an average of $-0.32$ on the remaining five, with cross-model agreement up to Krippendorff's $α= 0.85$. The composite behavioral score, used here only for cross-episode reporting, correlates at $ρ= 0.72$ with realized 20-day Sharpe ratio from offline backtesting. Closing the loop, the framework converts deficient per-dimension scores into a credit-assigned penalty term added to the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) reward. Three short fine-tuning cycles, all confined to the validation period, produce on the held-out 2017-2025 test period a one-day MAPE reduction from 0.61% to 0.54% (an 11.5% relative reduction; $p<0.001$, Cohen's $d=0.31$), a directional accuracy increase from 71% to 74%, and an 18% Sharpe ratio improvement (95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]), with gains concentrated in high-volatility episodes where the original system was most behaviorally deficient. Results are from offline backtesting and do not address effects specific to live deployment.

</details>


### [303] [Adaptive Selection of LoRA Components in Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05769)
*Myoungjun Kim,Sangwoo Park,Yoseob Han,Jin-Hyun Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AS-LoRA，一种针对差分隐私联邦学习中LoRA微调的自适应框架，通过层-wise和轮次-wise的自适应更新选择以及曲率感知评分，消除了聚合误差，提升了精度和收敛速度，且不增加隐私成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私联邦大模型LoRA微调中，LoRA的乘法结构导致聚合误差，DP噪声进一步放大该误差，损害稳定性和精度。现有方法对所有层和所有通信轮次采用统一或固定调度的更新模式，忽略了LoRA两个因子的结构不对称性和训练的动态性。

Method: AS-LoRA通过三个维度进行自适应：(i) 层-wise自由度，每层独立选择激活组件；(ii) 轮次-wise自适应性，选择随通信轮次动态更新；(iii) 曲率感知评分，基于损失函数的二阶近似。理论上消除了层绑定调度的重构误差下界，加速收敛，隐式偏向平坦极小值，且不增加隐私开销。

Result: 在GLUE、SQuAD、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上，在严格DP预算和非IID分区下，AS-LoRA相比联邦LoRA基线在GLUE上提升最高+7.5个百分点，MNLI-mm上提升+12.5个百分点，以33-180倍更低的聚合成本匹配或超越基于SVD的聚合方法，通信开销可忽略。

Conclusion: AS-LoRA通过自适应更新选择有效解决了DP联邦LoRA中的聚合误差问题，在精度、收敛速度、计算效率和隐私保护方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Differentially private federated fine-tuning of large models with LoRA suffers from aggregation error caused by LoRA's multiplicative structure, which is further amplified by DP noise and degrades both stability and accuracy. Existing remedies apply a single update mode uniformly across all layers and all communication rounds (or alternate them on a fixed schedule), ignoring both the structural asymmetry between the two LoRA factors and the round-wise dynamics of training. We propose AS-LoRA, an adaptive framework defined by three axes (i) layer-wise freedom, in which each layer independently selects its active component, (ii) round-wise adaptivity, in which the selection updates over communication rounds, and (iii) a curvature-aware score derived from a second-order approximation of the loss. Theoretically, AS-LoRA eliminates the reconstruction-error floor of layer-tied schedules, accelerates convergence, implicitly biases solutions toward flatter minima, and incurs no additional privacy cost. Across GLUE, SQuAD, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under strict DP budgets and non-IID partitions, AS-LoRA improves over the federated LoRA baselines by up to $+7.5$ pp on GLUE and $+12.5$ pp on MNLI-mm for example, while matching or exceeding SVD-based aggregation methods at $33\text{--}180 \times$ lower aggregation cost and with negligible communication overhead. Code for the proposed method is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/as_lora-F75F/.

</details>


### [304] [Revealing Modular Gradient Noise Imbalance in LLMs: Calibrating Adam via Signal-to-Noise Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05794)
*Ziqing Wen,Zhouyang Liu,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Li Shen,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoLS方法，通过估计模块级信噪比自动调整Adam优化器的模块学习率，无需手动调参，在LLM训练中加快收敛并提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的异构模块组成导致梯度异质性，现有自适应优化器（如Adam）未显式考虑模块级差异，手动调整模块学习率代价高且难以泛化。

Method: 分析Adam在高噪声模块中的噪声阻尼行为，提出MoLS，通过估计模块级信噪比（SNR）自动缩放Adam更新步长，实现模块级学习率的自动化分配。

Result: 在多个LLM训练基准上，MoLS加速收敛、提升泛化，性能媲美精心调参的模块特定学习率方案，且兼容内存高效训练算法。

Conclusion: MoLS是一种无需手动调参的自动化模块级学习率分配方法，能有效应对LLM训练中的梯度异质性挑战。

Abstract: The impressive performance of large language models (LLMs) arises from their massive scale and heterogeneous module composition. However, this structural heterogeneity introduces additional optimization challenges. While adaptive optimizers such as Adam(W) provide per-parameter adaptivity, they do not explicitly account for module-level gradient heterogeneity, resulting in slower convergence, suboptimal performance, or training instability. Existing approaches typically rely on manually tuned module-specific learning rates or specific optimization strategies, which are computationally costly and difficult to generalize across tasks or models. To establish a more principled approach, we first analyze the noise-damping behavior of Adam in high-noise modules and introduce \textbf{Module-wise Learning Rate Scaling via SNR (MoLS)}. MoLS estimates module-level SNRs to scale Adam updates, allowing automated module-wise learning rate allocation without manual tuning. Empirical results through multiple LLM training benchmarks demonstrate that MoLS improves convergence speed and generalization, achieving performance comparable to carefully tuned module-specific learning rates, while remaining compatible with memory-efficient training algorithms.

</details>


### [305] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对变分自编码器后验坍缩问题，提出了一种可测量的常量坍缩边界：当编码器的对齐损失低于教师互信息且留有安全裕度时，编码器必然不是输入无关的常量。实验证明该边界可检测、可预防、可恢复，但仅针对输入无关的常量坍缩这一特定失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验坍缩的现有诊断方法（如小KL项、强解码器、弱潜码使用）仅能识别症状，无法定义坍缩的边界。本文旨在为一种具体的失效模式——输入无关的常量坍缩——建立精确的可量化阈值。

Method: 推导出理论恒等式：对于任意固定的非常量教师分布T(·|x)，最优常量学生的对齐损失等于教师互信息I_T(X;T)。据此构建"原始证书"（raw certificate）：若潜码编码器的对齐损失低于该值且留有安全裕度，则编码器必非输入无关的常量。在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200上通过逐种子教师搜索进行实验验证。

Result: CIFAR-100实验：完整训练保持在证书边界的安全侧；移除对齐损失使原始证书落入常量学生区间；从坍缩检查点恢复对齐后可重建证书。Tiny-ImageNet-200实验中，三种独立搜索的教师均呈现相同的"预防-坍缩-恢复"模式。标准VAE基线方法（包括保留重构质量或事后可预测性的方法）在原始证书下均为阴性。

Conclusion: 该证书为您提供了一种窄但严格的保证：它认证了经过匹配的非常量教师相对变化通过潜通路传递，而非声称所有形式的后验坍缩都已被排除。该方法是可操作的，能够检测、预防并修复输入无关的常量坍缩。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [306] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOPE算法，利用与演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段的长度，在25个连续控制任务上最高提升45.6%性能并减少22倍计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先验数据融入在线强化学习虽能加速训练，但面临高计算成本与多阶段训练管道的权衡；固定长度的稳定化阶段虽计算高效但需要任务相关的手动调参，否则会浪费先验知识或导致严重过拟合。

Method: 提出SOPE算法，使用演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动化早停机制，在保留验证集上评估评论家在当前策略动作分布下的表现，当分布外收益饱和时立即停止梯度更新，无需手动调度调参。

Result: 在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上，SOPE将基线性能提升最高45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs减少高达22倍，有效平衡了样本效率与计算效率的权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举的更新调度更有效。

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


### [307] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）的复合次数对高维向量旋转质量的影响。证明两次RHT可使单坐标分布逼近高斯（误差O(d^{-1/2})），在梯度压缩等任务中可替代均匀随机旋转；三次RHT可保证坐标协方差衰减，满足向量量化的需求；并提出一种线性时间检测方法，动态调整RHT次数以应对实际非对抗性输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转（URR）是现代量化方法（梯度压缩、推理加速、KV缓存压缩、模型权重量化、近似最近邻搜索）中的常见预处理步骤，实践中常用随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）替代URR以保持正交性并支持快速实现。然而，RHT在最坏情况输入下性能不佳——单个坐标分布与理论分布（shifted beta/高斯）差距较大。因此，需要研究如何通过复合多次RHT来弥合与URR之间的性能差距。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明两次RHT后，归一化旋转向量中每个固定坐标的边缘分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下均以O(d^{-1/2})的误差逼近标准高斯分布。2）应用分析：将上述界限代入DRIVE和QUIC-FL等现代压缩方案，证明两次RHT可渐近匹配URR性能。3）向量量化（VQ）分析：证明三次RHT可导致坐标协方差衰减，使得任何针对URR优化的固定有界多维VQ码本在使用三次RHT时具有相同的期望误差（加上随维度消失的附加项）。4）动态适应：提出一种线性时间O(d)的输入矩检测方法，在运行时动态调整RHT次数。

Result: 两次RHT在单坐标边际分布上以O(d^{-1/2})误差逼近高斯，在DRIVE和QUIC-FL中渐近匹配URR性能；三次RHT确保坐标协方差衰减，使VQ码本的期望误差与URR一致（附加项随维度消失）。动态检测方法可在实际非对抗性输入中自适应选择RHT次数。

Conclusion: 通过适当增加RHT复合次数（两次用于梯度压缩等任务，三次用于向量量化），可以在最坏情况下弥合RHT与URR之间的性能差距，同时保持RHT的计算效率优势。动态适应机制进一步提升了实际应用中的灵活性。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [308] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（4,218次实验）系统评估了合成数据在时间序列预测中的作用，发现效果高度依赖架构：通道混合模型（如TimesNet、iTransformer）在多数情况下受益，而通道独立模型（如DLinear、PatchTST）性能持续下降。平均来看，数据增强在67%的试验中有害。仅在低资源场景（10%数据）下，TimesNet结合合成数据能超越全数据基线。研究还发现季节性-趋势生成器最可靠，硬课程切换策略会显著恶化性能（MSE增加24%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在语言模型训练中已被广泛应用，但在时间序列预测领域的作用尚不明确，缺乏系统性的实证研究来指导实践。

Method: 设计大规模实证研究框架，包含9个实验组、4,218次运行，跨5种架构（TimesNet、iTransformer、DLinear、PatchTST等）、4种合成信号和7个数据集进行系统评估。

Result: 通道混合模型多数受益，通道独立模型持续退化；低资源场景下TimesNet结合合成数据可超越全数据基线；仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠；硬课程切换导致24% MSE劣化。

Conclusion: 提出了具体可操作的使用合成数据的指导原则：对通道混合架构使用合成增强、采用渐进退火调度策略、将低资源增强视为架构和数据集依赖的任务。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [309] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SelectiveRM框架，基于最优传输理论，通过联合一致性差异和部分传输的质量松弛机制，实现对RLHF中噪声偏好的鲁棒去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在RLHF中至关重要，但真实数据集中不可避免地存在噪声偏好，传统训练目标容易过拟合这些错误，而现有去噪方法依赖的同质噪声假设无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性。

Method: 1) 提出联合一致性差异(Joint Consistency Discrepancy)来对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布；2) 引入部分传输(Partial Transport)的质量松弛(Mass Relaxation)机制，允许模型自主排除与语义一致性矛盾的噪声偏好样本，解决严格质量守恒导致的离群点拟合问题。

Result: 理论证明了SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧的上界；实验表明在多个benchmark上显著优于当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的部分松弛机制能有效处理RLHF中复杂、非结构化的偏好噪声，提升奖励模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [310] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TFM-Retouche，一种架构无关的输入空间残差适配器，通过在输入空间学习微小残差校正来对齐预训练表格基础模型的归纳偏置，无需修改模型内部结构。在TabArena-Lite基准上，基于TabICLv2的实例化以轻量级微调和集成方式位列排行榜第一，Elo评分提升+56。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFM）在推理时归纳偏置固定，适配特定数据集需全量微调（计算昂贵）或参数高效微调（需适配内部架构），且权重空间微调对精度和校准的改进效果存在争议。

Method: 提出TFM-Retouche，一个轻量级输入空间残差适配器，在输入空间学习小的残差校正；通过冻结的TFM进行端到端训练；设置训练后身份保护机制，当适配在验证集上无帮助时回退到原始TFM。

Result: 在TabArena-Lite（51个数据集，涵盖二分类、多分类和回归）上，TabICLv2-Retouche在排行榜上排名第一，Elo总分比冻结的TabICLv2提升+56，处于预测质量与训练/推理时间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 输入空间适配是权重空间微调的有效替代方案，能保持架构无关性并提升TFM的零样本性能，同时保持较低的计算开销。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [311] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MTG-Causal-RL基准测试平台，基于《Magic: The Gathering》构建，包含3077维部分观测、478动作的掩码离散动作空间、五个标准赛制套牌类型、三种奖励方案和显式结构因果模型(SCM)。实验对比了随机、启发式、掩码PPO、因果世界模型PPO等基线，并提出了CGFA-PPO（因果图因子优势PPO）作为参考因果智能体。掩码PPO和CGFA-PPO达到具有竞争力的分布内胜率；每个因子的校准轨迹和留一法跨套牌迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法单独揭示的诊断结构。该基准为因果强化学习、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了统一测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果强化学习缺乏能够同时结合序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准测试平台，导致相关研究无法在这些关键维度上进行标准化评估。

Method: 基于《Magic: The Gathering》构建Gymnasium基准测试平台，包含3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散空间、五种标准赛制套牌、三种奖励方案和手工指定的SCM。提出CGFA-PPO算法，使用SCM中胜率概率的父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并引入干预校准损失。采用配对种子、配对bootstrap置信区间和Holm-Bonferroni校正进行统计比较。

Result: 掩码PPO和CGFA-PPO在分布内数据上达到具有竞争力的胜率并超过随机基线。每个因子的校准轨迹和留一法跨套牌迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法单独揭示的诊断结构。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL通过将战略丰富的部分可观测领域与显式因果接口和统计协议相结合，为因果强化学习、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，能够同时评估掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨套牌类型的结构迁移和基于SCM的策略可审计性等当前基准无法共同提出的研究问题。

Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

</details>


### [312] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: nGPT通过约束权重和隐藏表示到单位超球面上，天然对低精度（4-bit）算术更鲁棒，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，并在1.2B密集模型和高达3B/30B的混合MoE模型上得到验证。其鲁棒性源于超球面约束增强了点积中信号的弱正相关积累，提高了有效信噪比，且随隐藏维度增大优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型训练中使用4-bit低精度是提升效率的关键，但标准架构在低精度下模型质量下降，需要引入随机Hadamard变换和逐张量缩放等额外干预来保持质量，增加了复杂性和成本。

Method: 采用nGPT架构，将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上，利用超球面约束增强点积中元素级乘积的弱正相关信号积累，同时保持噪声不相关，从而提升有效信噪比，实现端到端NVFP4低位宽训练。

Result: 在1.2B密集模型和最高3B/30B的混合Mamba-Transformer MoE模型上验证了该方法无需额外干预即可稳定训练。超球面约束下的信号随隐藏维度增大呈现更强的正相关积累，噪声保持去相关，有效信噪比更高，损失景观更平坦。

Conclusion: nGPT架构天然适合低精度训练，其优势随模型规模（隐藏维度）增大而增强，在大规模模型上具有显著的扩展优势，为高效大模型训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [313] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT通过将RTG信息注入状态表示而非作为独立token，将序列长度缩短1/3，在D4RL基准上超越标准DT且效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Decision Transformer中RTG作为标量token与丰富的状态/动作向量消耗相同计算预算，且自注意力复杂度随序列长度二次增长，存在计算浪费。

Method: 将RTG信息在序列建模前注入到状态表示中，从而从自回归序列中移除RTG token，使Transformer仅处理(状态, 动作)的紧凑序列。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，SlimDT超越标准DT，性能与现有最佳方法相当。

Conclusion: 将稀疏的条件信号从信息丰富的序列中解耦，既能获得计算收益，又能提升任务性能。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [314] [Listwise Policy Optimization: Group-based RLVR as Target-Projection on the LLM Response Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06139)
*Yun Qu,Qi Wang,Yixiu Mao,Heming Zou,Yuhang Jiang,Yingyue Li,Wutong Xu,Lizhou Cai,Weijie Liu,Clive Bai,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO)，通过显式地将策略投影到响应单纯形上的目标分布，替代了隐式的组相对策略梯度方法，在多个推理任务上实现了更优的训练性能、优化稳定性和响应多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的策略梯度方法虽被广泛用于LLM后训练，但其目标分布是隐式的，缺乏清晰的几何解释，限制了优化效率和稳定性。

Method: LPO将邻近策略优化目标限制在响应单纯形上，通过显式定义目标分布并执行精确的散度最小化投影，实现解耦的投影步骤，支持灵活选择散度度量。

Result: 在多种推理任务和LLM骨干上，LPO在匹配目标条件下一致优于典型策略梯度基线，同时保持优化稳定性和响应多样性；投影梯度具有有界、零和、自修正特性。

Conclusion: LPO框架提供了序列化目标的单调改进保证，通过显式目标-投影机制提升了RLVR训练的几何可解释性和实际性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for large language models (LLMs) post-training to incentivize reasoning capacity. Among existing recipes, group-based policy gradient is prevalent, which samples a group of responses per prompt and updates the policy via group-relative advantage signals. This work reveals that these optimization strategies share a common geometric structure: each implicitly defines a target distribution on the response simplex and projects toward it via first-order approximation. Building on this insight, we propose Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO) to explicitly conduct the target-projection, which demystifies the implicit target by restricting the proximal RL objective to the response simplex, and then projects the policy via exact divergence minimization. This framework provides (i) monotonic improvement on the listwise objective with bounded, zero-sum, and self-correcting projection gradients, and (ii) flexibility in divergence selection with distinct structural properties through the decoupled projection step. On diverse reasoning tasks and LLM backbones, LPO consistently improves training performance over typical policy gradient baselines under matched targets, while intrinsically preserving optimization stability and response diversity.

</details>


### [315] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上统一了目标条件强化学习（GCRL）与互信息技能学习（MISL）两类方法，将其归入控制最大化框架，证明了不同GCRL公式间的不等价性，并建立了MISL目标与下游任务灵敏度之间的精确对应关系，为无监督预训练奠定了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督预训练在GCRL中取得了显著实证成功，但MISL方法为何能支持下游目标达成任务的理论基础尚不清晰。GCRL和MISL均为涵盖多种变体的术语，不同公式使用不同的性能度量标准，这使得理论分析困难。

Method: 将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化（control maximization）的实例；识别三类典型GCRL公式并证明其根本不等价；将MISL目标解释为类似于目标灵敏度的技能灵敏度度量；推导MISL目标与下游目标灵敏度之间的上界关系。

Result: 三类GCRL公式即使在同一环境中也会产生不相容的最优策略，但它们共享一个共同解释：良好的目标条件策略应使未来轨迹对命令目标高度敏感。MISL目标受限于特定公式的下游目标灵敏度，且对于每种GCRL公式，存在对应的MISL目标，使得更丰富的技能带来更高的下游目标灵敏度。

Conclusion: 建立了RL预训练的理论基础，为实践者根据下游任务类型选择合适的预训练目标提供了指导，具有重要的实际意义。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [316] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）框架，通过在连续流模型中引入镜像下降熵最大化和混合行为先验机制，解决了离线强化学习中生成式策略的流行度偏差和支持受限问题，在稀疏奖励连续控制任务上达到或超越先前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAM方法虽然通过连续伴随方法稳定了策略优化，但受限于固定的行为分布，导致流行度偏差（压制低密度区域的高奖励动作）和支持受限（限制离流形探索）两大问题。简单的残差高斯策略补丁又回到了单峰分布的表达能力瓶颈。

Method: ME-AM框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以从离线数据中提取最优策略；(2) 混合行为先验，数学上拓宽几何支持以覆盖分布外的高奖励区域，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 在多样化的稀疏奖励连续控制环境中，ME-AM相比先前的最先进方法展现出具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM通过统一的连续流框架有效解决了生成式策略在离线RL中的支持受限和偏差问题，为复杂多模态行为的学习提供了更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [317] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈感知的上下文黑箱优化（FICBO），通过预训练Transformer同时利用历史观测和廉价辅助反馈来加速优化，并能自适应评估反馈源的可靠性，在多种任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法要么只能处理单一反馈源，缺乏泛化能力；要么假设仅优化历史可用，无法利用丰富的辅助信息（如专家知识、模拟器、预训练预测器等）。

Method: 提出结构化反馈先验，建模反馈源在可访问性、相关性和失真度上的差异；基于此先验预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer，使其在测试时能通过对比观测目标值与辅助信号来在线估计源可靠性，从而改进查询选择。

Result: 在合成和真实任务上，FICBO能有效利用有益反馈，同时对弱或误导性源保持鲁棒性，优于其他基线方法。实验还展示了模型对测试时反馈源的感知能力，揭示了其可解释性和决策过程。

Conclusion: FICBO通过上下文学习实现了对多源辅助反馈的灵活利用与可靠性自适应评估，为融合异构先验信息的黑箱优化提供了一种有效且可解释的范式。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [318] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了对比学习框架下的极限识别与极限生成问题。学习者只能看到满足h(x)≠h(y)的无序对{x,y}，而不知道具体哪个是正例。作者给出了无噪声环境下的三个结果：对比可识别类的精确刻画（Angluin条件的几何细化）、对比闭包维度（统一刻画对比生成及其样本复杂度）、以及对比生成与正例识别的严格层次关系。在有限对抗破坏下，存在可被对比对识别但无法被正例识别的类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极限识别/生成模型仅关注正例或完全标注数据，但许多自然监督信号本质上是关系性的（如对比对），而非单个样本的标签。现有理论无法处理这种仅提供样本对是否异类的关系型监督信号，因此需要建立对比学习在极限模型下的理论基础。

Method: 引入共同交叉图（common crossing graph）作为统一技术对象，编码成对歧义性、族级生成障碍和破坏缺陷。利用图论覆盖与关联语言，推导出对比可识别性的精确条件（Angluin tell-tale条件的几何细化）、对比闭包维度（对比版本的闭包维度），并通过构造严格的反例建立层次关系。

Result: (1) 无噪声下：对比可识别类有精确刻画；对比闭包维度统一刻画对比生成并给出紧样本复杂度；对比生成与正例识别互不可比。(2) 有限对抗破坏下：存在可被对比对识别（任意有限破坏预算）但无法被正例识别（哪怕一个破坏）的类别，形成严格反转。

Conclusion: 对比对提供的关系型监督信号在极限学习模型中具有独特的理论优势：在无噪声时与正例识别互不可比，在对抗破坏下甚至比正例识别更鲁棒。共同交叉图是统一理解这些现象的核心工具。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [319] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆注入（Memory Inception, MI），一种无需训练的大模型操控方法，通过在选定层插入文本派生的KV缓存（而非全层提示缓存）实现潜在注意力空间操控。MI在个性操控任务中实现最佳操控-漂移权衡，在中途行为切换任务中实现最高对齐度，在结构化推理任务中以118倍KV存储压缩超越显式提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型操控方法存在两难：指令提示虽控制力强但缓存冗余、交互冗长；激活操控（如CAA）虽紧凑但操控力弱且不支持结构化提示。作者希望设计一种兼具提示强度与激活操控紧凑性的无需训练方法。

Method: 记忆注入（MI）是一种无需训练的方法，在潜在注意力空间进行操控。具体做法是将文本导出的键值（KV）缓存仅插入选定层（而非所有层），将操控视为选择性KV分配，模型仅在路由到这些潜在槽位时触发操控效果。

Result: 1) 个性操控任务中实现最佳操控-漂移权衡，优于CAA且与提示竞争；2) 支持中途对话行为切换（不重写可见对话历史），在Qwen3上达最高后切换对齐度；3) 结构化推理任务（HARDMath、PHYSICS）在10/12个学科×模式组合中优于显式提示，同时实现高达118倍的KV存储压缩。

Conclusion: MI是一种强大的操控方法，特别适用于持久性、结构化或显式提示成本高昂的场景，为无需训练的大模型操控提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [320] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯平滑的软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG）框架，通过平滑贝尔曼方程定义新的动作价值函数，消除对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，即使Q函数非光滑也能保证策略梯度良定义。基于此框架实现Soft DDPG算法，在离散奖励等非光滑环境中显著优于标准DDPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DPG依赖评论家对动作的可微性，但在稀疏或离散奖励等实际控制问题中，该假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不良和学习不稳定。

Method: 通过高斯平滑构建平滑贝尔曼方程，定义新的动作价值函数，推导软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），消除对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，确保梯度在非光滑Q函数下仍良定义。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准及其离散奖励变体上，Soft DDPG在密集奖励下保持竞争力，在大部分离散奖励环境中显著优于标准DDPG。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了非光滑奖励下DPG梯度定义不良的问题，为实际控制中常见的非光滑环境提供了稳定且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [321] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态推荐系统中逃避式推广攻击的跨模态梯度不匹配问题，提出UAT-MC方法，通过梯度对齐机制实现跨模态扰动同步，有效提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统易受逃避式推广攻击威胁，现有防御主要针对单模态和投毒式攻击，对逃避式攻击研究不足；多用户推广场景下存在跨模态梯度不匹配现象，导致攻击效果稀释和鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出UAT-MC方法：1）将逃避式攻击中未知目标物品问题转化为将所有物品视为潜在目标进行无目标对抗训练；2）引入梯度对齐机制显式修正视觉与文本模态间的梯度不匹配，实现跨模态扰动同步，最大化对抗强度。

Result: 大量实验表明UAT-MC在显著提升对推广攻击鲁棒性的同时，在防御-准确率权衡下保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统中逃避式推广攻击的防御难题，为多模态鲁棒推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [322] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了前向-前向（FF）训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：在累积好度的FF变体中，后续层会继承前面层已部分完成的任务。作者形式化证明了在softplus FF准则下，到达第d块的类别区分梯度会随前面块累积的正向边距呈指数衰减。针对此问题，提出了三种局部补救方法（逐块、硬度门控、深度缩放），在不依赖反向传播梯度的情况下恢复当前层的分离度量。在CIFAR-10/100上，这些方法在深层实现了4-45倍的层分离统计提升，而精度变化不到1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet实验验证了层健康诊断与最终精度之间的定性差距。消融实验表明，架构和数据增强选择对最终精度的影响远大于训练规则修改。结论是：累积搭便车是一种真实且可修复的优化病理，但在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集上，它并非限制最终精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向-前向（FF）训练中，累积好度变体存在一个未被充分认识的问题：后续层会"搭便车"，即依赖前面层已经部分完成的分类任务，导致深层未能学到有意义的表示。作者希望形式化并量化这一现象，并探索不依赖反向传播的局部补救方法。

Method: 1) 形式化分析：证明在softplus FF准则下，到达块d的梯度随前面块累积正向边距呈指数衰减；2) 提出三种局部补救方法：逐块（per-block）、硬度门控（hardness-gated）、深度缩放（depth-scaled）；3) 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny ImageNet上进行实验验证，包括层分离统计、精度对比及消融实验。

Result: 1) 三种补救方法在深层实现了4-45倍的层分离统计提升；2) 对于非退化训练过程，精度变化小于1个百分点；3) Tiny ImageNet上验证了层健康诊断与最终精度之间的定性差距；4) 消融实验显示架构和数据增强对精度的影响远大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是FF训练中一个真实且可修复的优化病理现象，但在所研究的设定下，它并不是限制最终精度的主导因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [323] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pro-KLShampoo优化器，通过利用KL-Shampoo预条件子的"尖峰-平坦"谱结构（主导特征值+均匀尾部分布），在追踪的低维子空间上保留完整谱结构，其余方向采用正交化，从而在GPT-2/LLaMA预训练中超越KL-Shampoo，实现更低的验证损失、更少的内存占用和更快的训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子在不同层和训练阶段表现出"尖峰-平坦"的特征值谱结构（少量主导特征值后跟随近似均匀的尾部），这一观察尚未被充分利用来设计更高效的优化器。

Method: 1) 观察到KL-Shampoo的Kronecker因子具有尖峰-平坦谱结构；2) 将其中一个Kronecker因子限制为参数化族：在追踪的r维子空间上保留完整谱结构，剩余n-r维方向共享单一特征值；3) 对剩余方向应用正交化；4) 证明该正交化等价于完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M和LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在所有测试的子空间秩下，在验证损失、峰值每GPU内存和达到各损失水平的墙钟时间上均一致优于KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: 通过利用预条件子的谱结构先验，可以在保持KL-Shampoo理论优势的同时显著提升计算效率和内存效率，为LLM预训练优化器设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [324] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约束优化和对偶理论的模型级联决策框架，证明了两模型级联的成本-质量前沿的逐段凹性，揭示了预算约束与质量约束问题间的对偶关系。针对k模型池，刻画了确定性两模型阈值级联的前沿为成对级联的点态包络。实验表明，全固定链性能劣于成对包络，而轻量级预生成路由器因避免廉价模型生成成本而表现更优，说明级联性能主要受结构成本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量前沿几何结构的理论指导，导致在实际部署中难以系统性地导航成本与质量间的权衡。

Method: 采用约束优化与对偶理论构建决策框架；证明两模型级联成本-质量前沿的逐段凹性及影子价格互逆关系；推导k模型级联的一阶条件；在5个基准、8个模型上实证验证。

Result: 两模型级联前沿具逐段凹性；k模型级联中阈值级联的前沿为成对包络；全固定链弱于成对包络；预生成路由器优于最佳级联策略。

Conclusion: 级联性能主要受结构成本（级联在决策前需支付廉价模型生成成本）限制，而非中间阶段数量不足。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [325] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AuxPath-FM 框架，通过引入服从任意分布的辅助变量 η 来泛化条件流匹配，允许设计多样化概率路径（如高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散 Rademacher 分布），并支持标签引导生成等特殊任务，理论证明了连续性方程和训练目标的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配方法将辅助变量限制为高斯噪声，缺乏灵活性。本文旨在提出一个更通用的框架，允许辅助变量服从任意分布，从而设计多样化的概率路径并支持更广泛的应用场景。

Method: 提出 AuxPath-FM，将生成轨迹扩展为 X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η，其中 η 可服从任意分布（高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、Rademacher 等）。理论上证明该构造保留了连续性方程，且训练目标与边缘公式一致。

Result: 理论证明了所提框架的合理性和一般性。不同辅助分布（高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、Rademacher）各自提供了独特的几何特性。此外，通过将结构化语义信息编码到辅助分布中，实现了标签引导生成等专门任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM 为概率路径设计提供了原则性且通用的基础，兼具理论一般性和实践灵活性，适用于多样化的生成建模任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation.
